Homage to the Blessed One, the Worthy One, the Fully Awakened Buddha ^T-1

# In the Abhidhamma Piṭaka ^0

## The Enumeration of Phenomena ^1-0

### The Matrix ^1-M-0

#### The Matrix of Sets of Three ^1-M-1-0

(a) Wholesome states.
(b) Unwholesome states.
(c) Indeterminate states. ^1-M1-1

(a) States associated with pleasant feeling.
(b) States associated with painful feeling.
(c) States associated with feeling that is neither painful nor pleasant. ^1-M1-2

(a) States that are results.
(b) States that produce results.
(c) States that neither are results nor produce results. ^1-M1-3

(a) States produced by past action and subject to clinging [variant: upādinnupādāniyā (Syā.)].
(b) States not produced by past action but subject to clinging.
(c) States neither produced by past action nor subject to clinging [variant: anupādinnānupādāniyā (Syā.)]. ^1-M1-4

(a) States that are defiled and subject to defilement.
(b) States that are undefiled but subject to defilement.
(c) States that are undefiled and not subject to defilement [variant: asaṃkiliṭṭhāsaṃkilesikā (Syā.)]. ^1-M1-5

(a) States with applied thought and sustained thought.
(b) States without applied thought but with sustained thought only.
(c) States without applied thought and without sustained thought [variant: avitakkāvicārā (Syā.)]. ^1-M1-6

(a) States accompanied by rapture.
(b) States accompanied by happiness.
(c) States accompanied by equanimity. ^1-M1-7

(a) States to be abandoned by seeing.
(b) States to be abandoned by development.
(c) States to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development. ^1-M1-8

(a) States whose roots are to be abandoned by seeing.
(b) States whose roots are to be abandoned by development.
(c) States whose roots are to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development. ^1-M1-9

(a) States leading to accumulation.
(b) States leading away from accumulation.
(c) States leading neither to nor away from accumulation [variant: nevācayagāmino nāpacayagāmino (Syā.)]. ^1-M1-10

(a) States of one in training.
(b) States of one beyond training.
(c) States of one neither in training nor beyond training [variant: nevasekkhānāsekkhā (Sī., Syā.)]. ^1-M1-11

(a) Limited states.
(b) Exalted states.
(c) Immeasurable states. ^1-M1-12

(a) States with a limited object.
(b) States with an exalted object.
(c) States with an immeasurable object. ^1-M1-13

(a) Inferior states.
(b) Middling states.
(c) Superior states. ^1-M1-14

(a) States fixed in wrongness.
(b) States fixed in rightness.
(c) States that are not fixed. ^1-M1-15

(a) States with the path as their object.
(b) States with the path as their root.
(c) States with the path as their dominant factor. ^1-M1-16

(a) Arisen states.
(b) States that have not arisen.
(c) States that are bound to arise. ^1-M1-17

(a) Past states.
(b) Future states.
(c) Present states. ^1-M1-18

(a) States with a past object.
(b) States with a future object.
(c) States with a present object. ^1-M1-19

(a) One's own states.
(b) Others' states.
(c) States both one's own and others'. ^1-M1-20

(a) States with one's own states as object.
(b) States with others' states as object.
(c) States with both one's own and others' states as object. ^1-M1-21

(a) States that are visible and make impact.
(b) States that are invisible but make impact.
(c) States that are invisible and make no impact [variant: anidassanāppaṭighā (Syā.)].
The matrix of sets of three. ^1-M1-22

### The Chapter on the Arising of Mind ^1-1-0

#### The Wholesome Mind of the Sense Sphere ^1-1-1-0

##### The Analysis of the Terms ^1-1-1-1-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge, with a visible form as its object, or a sound as its object, or a smell as its object, or a taste as its object, or a touch as its object, or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy [variant: viriyindriyaṃ (Sī., Syā.)], there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right thought, there is right effort, there is right mindfulness, there is right concentration, there is the power of faith, there is the power of energy [variant: viriyabalaṃ (Sī., Syā.)], there is the power of mindfulness, there is the power of concentration, there is the power of wisdom, there is the power of moral shame, there is the power of fear of wrongdoing, there is non-greed, there is non-hate, there is non-delusion, there is non-covetousness, there is non-ill-will, there is right view, there is moral shame, there is fear of wrongdoing, there is tranquillity of the mental body [variant: kāyappassaddhi (Syā.)], there is tranquillity of mind [variant: cittappassaddhi (Syā.)], there is lightness of the mental body, there is lightness of mind, there is pliancy of the mental body, there is pliancy of mind, there is workability of the mental body, there is workability of mind, there is proficiency of the mental body, there is proficiency of mind, there is uprightness of the mental body [variant: kāyujjukatā (Sī., Ka.)], there is uprightness of mind [variant: cittujjukatā (Sī., Ka.)], there is mindfulness, there is clear awareness, there is calm, there is insight, there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states. ^1-1

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-2

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is feeling at that time. ^1-3

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-4

What is intention at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended [variant: sañcetayitattaṃ (Syā.)] – this is intention at that time. ^1-5

What is mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is mind at that time. ^1-6

What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, right thought – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-7

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-8

What is rapture at that time? Whatever at that time is rapture, gladness, delight, rejoicing, mirth, merriment, satisfaction, elation, contentment of mind – this is rapture at that time. ^1-9

What is happiness at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is happiness at that time. ^1-10

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-11

What is the faculty of faith at that time? Whatever at that time is faith, trusting, confiding, full confidence, faith, the faculty of faith, the power of faith – this is the faculty of faith at that time. ^1-12

What is the faculty of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy [variant: viriyārambho (Sī., Syā.)], setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour [variant: ussoḷhi (Sī.)], strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort – this is the faculty of energy at that time. ^1-13

What is the faculty of mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness – this is the faculty of mindfulness at that time. ^1-14

What is the faculty of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is the faculty of concentration at that time. ^1-15

What is the faculty of wisdom at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is the faculty of wisdom at that time. ^1-16

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-17

What is the faculty of joy at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the faculty of joy at that time. ^1-18

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving [variant: irīyanā (Sī.)], continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time. ^1-19

What is right view at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is right view at that time. ^1-20

What is right thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, right thought – this is right thought at that time. ^1-21

What is right effort at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort – this is right effort at that time. ^1-22

What is right mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness – this is right mindfulness at that time. ^1-23

What is right concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is right concentration at that time. ^1-24

What is the power of faith at that time? Whatever at that time is faith, trusting, confiding, full confidence, faith, the faculty of faith, the power of faith – this is the power of faith at that time. ^1-25

What is the power of mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness – this is the power of mindfulness at that time. ^1-27

What is the power of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is the power of concentration at that time. ^1-28

What is the power of wisdom at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is the power of wisdom at that time. ^1-29

What is the power of moral shame at that time? At that time, being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of moral shame at that time. ^1-30

What is the power of fear of wrongdoing at that time? At that time, fearing what one should fear, fearing engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of fear of wrongdoing at that time. ^1-31

What is non-greed at that time? Whatever at that time is non-greed, not being greedy, the state of not being greedy, non-lust, not lusting, the state of not lusting, non-covetousness, non-greed, the wholesome root – this is non-greed at that time. ^1-32

What is non-hate at that time? Whatever at that time is non-hate, not hating, the state of not hating [variant: adūsanā adūsitattaṃ (Syā.)], non-ill-will, absence of malice, non-hate, the wholesome root – this is non-hate at that time? ^1-33

What is non-delusion at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, non-delusion, the wholesome root – this is non-delusion at that time. ^1-34

What is non-covetousness at that time? Whatever at that time is non-greed, not being greedy, the state of not being greedy, non-lust, not lusting, the state of not lusting, non-covetousness, non-greed, the wholesome root – this is non-covetousness at that time. ^1-35

What is non-ill-will at that time? Whatever at that time is non-hate, not hating, the state of not hating, non-ill-will, absence of malice, non-hate, the wholesome root – this is non-ill-will at that time. ^1-36

What is right view at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is right view at that time. ^1-37

What is moral shame at that time? At that time, being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is moral shame at that time. ^1-38

What is tranquillity of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is tranquillity, full tranquillity [variant: paṭippassaddhi (Sī., Syā.)], calming, full calming [variant: paṭippassambhanā (Sī., Syā.)], the state of being fully calmed [variant: paṭippassambhitattaṃ (Sī., Syā.)] – this is tranquillity of the mental body at that time. ^1-40

What is lightness of the mental body at that time? What is pliancy of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is pliancy, softness, freedom from hardness, freedom from rigidity – this is pliancy of the mental body at that time. ^1-44

What is workability of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is workability, being workable, the condition of being workable – this is workability of the mental body at that time. ^1-46

What is workability of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is workability, being workable, the condition of being workable – this is workability of mind at that time. ^1-47

What is proficiency of the mental body at that time? What is uprightness of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is straightness, uprightness, freedom from deviousness, freedom from crookedness, freedom from twistedness – this is uprightness of the mental body at that time. ^1-50

What is clear awareness at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is clear awareness at that time. ^1-53

What is calm at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is calm at that time. ^1-54

What is insight at that time? What is non-distraction at that time? Or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states.

The first recitation section. ^1-57

##### The Section on Categories ^1-1-1-2-0

Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a five-factored absorption, there is a five-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact, there is one feeling, there is one perception, there is one intention, there is one mind, there is one aggregate of feeling, there is one aggregate of perception, there is one aggregate of mental formations, there is one aggregate of consciousness, there is one mind base, there is one faculty of mind, there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states. ^1-58

What are the four aggregates at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, the aggregate of consciousness. ^1-59

What is the aggregate of feeling at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the aggregate of feeling at that time. ^1-60

What is the aggregate of perception at that time? Whatever at that time is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is the aggregate of perception at that time. ^1-61

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right thought, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration, the power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of wisdom, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hate, non-delusion, non-covetousness, non-ill-will, right view, moral shame, fear of wrongdoing, tranquillity of the mental body, tranquillity of mind, lightness of the mental body, lightness of mind, pliancy of the mental body, pliancy of mind, workability of the mental body, workability of mind, proficiency of the mental body, proficiency of mind, uprightness of the mental body, uprightness of mind, mindfulness, clear awareness, calm, insight, exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except [variant: thapetvā (Ka.)] the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time. ^1-62

What is the aggregate of consciousness at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the aggregate of consciousness at that time.
These are the four aggregates at that time. ^1-63

What are the two sense bases at that time? The mind base, the mental-object base. ^1-64

What is the mind base at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the mind base at that time. ^1-65

What is the mental-object base at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – this is the mental-object base at that time.
These are the two sense bases at that time. ^1-66

What are the two elements at that time? The mind-consciousness element, the mental-object element. ^1-67

What is the mind-consciousness element at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the mind-consciousness element at that time. ^1-68

What is the mental-object element at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – this is the mental-object element at that time.
These are the two elements at that time. ^1-69

What are the three nutriments at that time? The nutriment of contact, the nutriment of mental intention, the nutriment of consciousness. ^1-70

What is the nutriment of contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is the nutriment of contact at that time. ^1-71

What is the nutriment of mental intention at that time? Whatever at that time is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is the nutriment of mental intention at that time. ^1-72

What is the nutriment of consciousness at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the nutriment of consciousness at that time.
These are the three nutriments at that time. ^1-73

What are the eight faculties at that time? The faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of mind, the faculty of joy, the faculty of life. ^1-74

What is the faculty of faith at that time? Whatever at that time is faith, trusting, confiding, full confidence, faith, the faculty of faith, the power of faith – this is the faculty of faith at that time. ^1-75

What is the faculty of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort – this is the faculty of energy at that time. ^1-76

What is the faculty of mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness – this is the faculty of mindfulness at that time. ^1-77

What is the faculty of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is the faculty of concentration at that time. ^1-78

What is the faculty of wisdom at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is the faculty of wisdom at that time. ^1-79

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-80

What is the faculty of joy at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the faculty of joy at that time. ^1-81

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time.
These are the eight faculties at that time. ^1-82

What is the five-factored absorption at that time? Applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, happiness, one-pointedness of mind. ^1-83

What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, right thought – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-84

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-85

What is rapture at that time? Whatever at that time is rapture, gladness, delight, rejoicing, mirth, merriment, satisfaction, elation, contentment of mind – this is rapture at that time. ^1-86

What is happiness at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is happiness at that time. ^1-87

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time.
This is the five-factored absorption at that time. ^1-88

What is the five-factored path at that time? Right view, right thought, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration. ^1-89

What is right view at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is right view at that time. ^1-90

What is right thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, right thought – this is right thought at that time. ^1-91

What is right effort at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort – this is right effort at that time. ^1-92

What is right mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness – this is right mindfulness at that time. ^1-93

What is right concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is right concentration at that time.
This is the five-factored path at that time. ^1-94

What are the seven powers at that time? The power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of wisdom, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing. ^1-95

What is the power of faith at that time? Whatever at that time is faith, trusting, confiding, full confidence, faith, the faculty of faith, the power of faith – this is the power of faith at that time. ^1-96

What is the power of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort – this is the power of energy at that time. ^1-97

What is the power of mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness – this is the power of mindfulness at that time. ^1-98

What is the power of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration – this is the power of concentration at that time. ^1-99

What is the power of wisdom at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is the power of wisdom at that time. ^1-100

What is the power of moral shame at that time? At that time, being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of moral shame at that time. ^1-101

What is the power of fear of wrongdoing at that time? At that time, fearing what one should fear, fearing engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of fear of wrongdoing at that time.
These are the seven powers at that time. ^1-102

What are the three roots at that time? Non-greed, non-hate, non-delusion. ^1-103

What is non-greed at that time? Whatever at that time is non-greed, not being greedy, the state of not being greedy, non-lust, not lusting, the state of not lusting, non-covetousness, non-greed, the wholesome root – this is non-greed at that time. ^1-104

What is non-hate at that time? Whatever at that time is non-hate, not hating, the state of not hating, non-ill-will, absence of malice, non-hate, the wholesome root – this is non-hate at that time. ^1-105

What is non-delusion at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding… non-delusion, investigation of states, right view – this is non-delusion at that time.
These are the three roots at that time. ^1-106

What is the one feeling at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the one feeling at that time. ^1-108

What is the one perception at that time? Whatever at that time is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is the one perception at that time. ^1-109

What is the one mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the one mind at that time. ^1-111

What is the one aggregate of feeling at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the one aggregate of feeling at that time. ^1-112

What is the one aggregate of perception at that time? Whatever at that time is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is the one aggregate of perception at that time. ^1-113

What is the one aggregate of consciousness at that time? What is the one mental-object base at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – this is the one mental-object base at that time. ^1-119

What is the one mental-object element at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – this is the one mental-object element at that time.
Or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states.

##### The Section on Emptiness ^1-1-1-3-0

Now at that time there are states, there are aggregates, there are sense bases, there are elements, there are nutriments, there are faculties, there is absorption, there is a path, there are powers, there are roots, there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is the aggregate of feeling, there is the aggregate of perception, there is the aggregate of mental formations, there is the aggregate of consciousness, there is the mind base, there is the faculty of mind, there is the mind-consciousness element, there is the mental-object base, there is the mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states. ^1-121

What are the states at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, the aggregate of consciousness – these are the states at that time. ^1-122

What are the aggregates at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, the aggregate of consciousness – these are the aggregates at that time. ^1-123

What are the sense bases at that time? The mind base, the mental-object base – these are the sense bases at that time. ^1-124

What are the elements at that time? The mind-consciousness element, the mental-object element – these are the elements at that time. ^1-125

What are the nutriments at that time? The nutriment of contact, the nutriment of mental intention, the nutriment of consciousness – these are the nutriments at that time. ^1-126

What are the faculties at that time? The faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of mind, the faculty of joy, the faculty of life – these are the faculties at that time. ^1-127

What is absorption at that time? Applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, happiness, one-pointedness of mind – this is absorption at that time. ^1-128

What is the path at that time? Right view, right thought, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration – this is the path at that time. ^1-129

What are the powers at that time? The power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of wisdom, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing – these are the powers at that time. ^1-130

What are the roots at that time? Non-greed, non-hate, non-delusion – these are the roots at that time. ^1-131

What is contact at that time… this is contact at that time. ^1-132

What is feeling at that time… this is feeling at that time. ^1-133

What is perception at that time… this is perception at that time. ^1-134

What is intention at that time… this is intention at that time? ^1-135

What is mind at that time… this is mind at that time. ^1-136

What is the aggregate of feeling at that time… this is the aggregate of feeling at that time. ^1-137

What is the aggregate of perception at that time… this is the aggregate of perception at that time. ^1-138

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time. ^1-139

What is the aggregate of consciousness at that time… this is the aggregate of consciousness at that time. ^1-140

What is the mind base at that time… this is the mind base at that time. ^1-141

What is the faculty of mind at that time… this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-142

What is the mind-consciousness element at that time… this is the mind-consciousness element at that time. ^1-143

What is the mental-object base at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – this is the mental-object base at that time. ^1-144

What is the mental-object element at that time? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – this is the mental-object element at that time.
Or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states.
The section on emptiness.
The first type of mind. ^1-145

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge, with prompting, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The second type of mind. ^1-146

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge, with a visible form as its object, or a sound as its object, or a smell as its object, or a taste as its object, or a touch as its object, or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right thought, there is right effort, there is right mindfulness, there is right concentration, there is the power of faith, there is the power of energy, there is the power of mindfulness, there is the power of concentration, there is the power of moral shame, there is the power of fear of wrongdoing, there is non-greed, there is non-hate, there is non-covetousness, there is non-ill-will, there is moral shame, there is fear of wrongdoing, there is tranquillity of the mental body, there is tranquillity of mind, there is lightness of the mental body, there is lightness of mind, there is pliancy of the mental body, there is pliancy of mind, there is workability of the mental body, there is workability of mind, there is proficiency of the mental body, there is proficiency of mind, there is uprightness of the mental body, there is uprightness of mind, there is mindfulness, there is calm, there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are seven faculties, there is a five-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are six powers, there are two roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-147

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of life, right thought, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration, the power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hate, non-covetousness, non-ill-will, moral shame, fear of wrongdoing, tranquillity of the mental body, tranquillity of mind, lightness of the mental body, lightness of mind, pliancy of the mental body, pliancy of mind, workability of the mental body, workability of mind, proficiency of the mental body, proficiency of mind, uprightness of the mental body, uprightness of mind, mindfulness, calm, exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states.
The third type of mind. ^1-148

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The fourth type of mind. ^1-149

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge, with a visible form as its object, or a sound as its object, or a smell as its object, or a taste as its object, or a touch as its object, or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right thought, there is right effort, there is right mindfulness, there is right concentration, there is the power of faith, there is the power of energy, there is the power of mindfulness, there is the power of concentration, there is the power of wisdom, there is the power of moral shame, there is the power of fear of wrongdoing, there is non-greed, there is non-hate, there is non-delusion, there is non-covetousness, there is non-ill-will, there is right view, there is moral shame, there is fear of wrongdoing, there is tranquillity of the mental body, there is tranquillity of mind, there is lightness of the mental body, there is lightness of mind, there is pliancy of the mental body, there is pliancy of mind, there is workability of the mental body, there is workability of mind, there is proficiency of the mental body, there is proficiency of mind, there is uprightness of the mental body, there is uprightness of mind, there is mindfulness, there is clear awareness, there is calm, there is insight, there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states. ^1-150

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-151

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is feeling at that time…. ^1-152

What is equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is equanimity at that time…. ^1-153

What is the faculty of equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is the faculty of equanimity at that time… or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a four-factored absorption, there is a five-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-154

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right thought, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration, the power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of wisdom, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hate, non-delusion, non-covetousness, non-ill-will, right view, moral shame, fear of wrongdoing, tranquillity of the mental body, tranquillity of mind, lightness of the mental body, lightness of mind, pliancy of the mental body, pliancy of mind, workability of the mental body, workability of mind, proficiency of the mental body, proficiency of mind, uprightness of the mental body, uprightness of mind, mindfulness, clear awareness, calm, insight, exertion, non-distraction.
Or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states.

Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of life, right thought, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration, the power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hate, non-covetousness, non-ill-will, moral shame, fear of wrongdoing, tranquillity of the mental body, tranquillity of mind, lightness of the mental body, lightness of mind, pliancy of the mental body, pliancy of mind, workability of the mental body, workability of mind, proficiency of the mental body, proficiency of mind, uprightness of the mental body, uprightness of mind, mindfulness, calm, exertion, non-distraction.

At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The eighth type of mind.
The eight great wholesome types of mind of the sense sphere.
The second recitation section. ^1-159

#### The Wholesome Mind of the Fine-Material Sphere ^1-1-2-0

##### The Fourfold Method ^1-1-2-1-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures, secluded from unwholesome states, enters and dwells in the first absorption [variant: paṭhamajjhānaṃ (Sī.)], which has applied thought and sustained thought, and rapture and happiness born of seclusion, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-160

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought, enters and dwells in the second absorption [variant: dutiyajjhānaṃ (Sī.)], which has inner confidence and unification of mind, is without applied thought and without sustained thought, and has rapture and happiness born of concentration, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a three-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-161

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states. ^1-162

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the fading away of rapture, dwells equanimous, mindful and clearly aware, experiences happiness with the body, and enters and dwells in the third absorption [variant: tatiyajjhānaṃ (Sī.)], of which the noble ones declare – "Equanimous and mindful, one dwells in happiness", using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a two-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-163

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states. ^1-164

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the abandoning of pleasure and the abandoning of pain, and with the earlier disappearance of joy and sadness, enters and dwells in the fourth absorption [variant: catutthajjhānaṃ (Sī.)], which is neither painful nor pleasant and has purity of mindfulness due to equanimity, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a two-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-165

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states.
The fourfold method. ^1-166

##### The Fivefold Method ^1-1-2-2-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, using earth as the meditation device – at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-167

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and enters and dwells in the second absorption, which is without applied thought but with sustained thought only, and has rapture and happiness born of concentration, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a four-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-168

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, sustained thought, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states. ^1-169

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… enters and dwells in the third absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a three-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-170

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states. ^1-171

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the fading away of rapture… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a two-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-172

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states. ^1-173

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption [variant: pañcamajjhānaṃ (Sī.)], using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life, there is right view, there is right effort… there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are eight faculties, there is a two-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-174

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, right view, right effort… exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states.
The fivefold method. ^1-175

##### The Four Modes of Progress ^1-1-2-3-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress [variant: dukkhāpaṭipadaṃ (in many editions)] and slow direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-176

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and quick direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-177

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with pleasant progress [variant: sukhāpaṭipadaṃ (in many editions)] and slow direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-178

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with pleasant progress and quick direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states…. ^1-179

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device… with painful progress and quick direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device… with pleasant progress and slow direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device… with pleasant progress and quick direct knowledge, using earth as the meditation device – at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The four modes of progress. ^1-180

##### The Four Kinds of Object ^1-1-2-4-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, limited and with a limited object, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-181

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, limited and with an immeasurable object, using earth as the meditation device – at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-182

##### The Eight Meditation Devices, Sixteenfold ^1-1-2-6-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, using water as the meditation device… using fire as the meditation device… using air as the meditation device… using blue as the meditation device… using yellow as the meditation device… using red as the meditation device… using white as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The eight meditation devices, sixteenfold. ^1-203

##### The Bases of Mastery over Limited Forms ^1-1-2-7-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and not perceiving material form in oneself, sees visible forms outside, limited ones, and, having mastered them, thinks "I know, I see", quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-204

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and not perceiving material form in oneself, sees visible forms outside, limited ones, and, having mastered them, thinks "I know, I see", with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption … the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-205
