At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and not perceiving material form in oneself, sees visible forms outside, yellow ones, yellow in colour, yellow in appearance, shining yellow… red ones, red in colour, red in appearance, shining red… white ones, white in colour, white in appearance, shining white, and, having mastered them, thinks "I know, I see", quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
These bases of mastery too are sixteenfold. ^1-247

##### The Three Liberations, Sixteenfold ^1-1-2-17-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and possessing material form, sees visible forms, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-248

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and not perceiving material form in oneself, sees visible forms outside, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-249

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the thought "beautiful", quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
These three liberations too are sixteenfold. ^1-250

##### The Four Absorptions of the Divine Abidings, Sixteenfold ^1-1-2-18-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-251

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… enters and dwells in the second absorption, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-252

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the fading away of rapture… enters and dwells in the third absorption, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-253

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-254

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and enters and dwells in the second absorption, which is without applied thought but with sustained thought only, and has rapture and happiness born of concentration, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-255

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… enters and dwells in the third absorption, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-256

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the fading away of rapture… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by loving-kindness, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-257

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, accompanied by compassion, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-258

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by compassion, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-259

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, accompanied by sympathetic joy, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-260

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by sympathetic joy, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-261

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by equanimity, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The four absorptions of the divine abidings [variant: brahmavihārajjhānāni (Sī., Syā.)], sixteenfold. ^1-262

##### The Absorption on the Unattractive, Sixteenfold ^1-1-2-19-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, accompanied by the perception of a bloated corpse, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-263

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, accompanied by the perception of a discoloured corpse… accompanied by the perception of a festering corpse… accompanied by the perception of a corpse cut apart… accompanied by the perception of a gnawed corpse… accompanied by the perception of a scattered corpse… accompanied by the perception of a hacked and scattered corpse… accompanied by the perception of a bleeding corpse… accompanied by the perception of a worm-infested corpse [variant: puḷuvakasaññāsahagataṃ (Ka.)] … accompanied by the perception of a skeleton, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The absorption on the unattractive [variant: asubhajjhānaṃ (Sī., Syā.)], sixteenfold.
The wholesome mind of the fine-material sphere. ^1-264

#### The Wholesome Mind of the Immaterial Sphere ^1-1-3-0

##### The Four Immaterial Absorptions [variant: arūpajjhānāni (Sī., Syā.)], Sixteenfold ^1-1-3-1-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and by completely passing beyond perceptions of material form, by the disappearance of perceptions of impact, by not attending to perceptions of diversity, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite space, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by equanimity, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-265

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and having completely passed beyond the base of infinite space, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite consciousness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by equanimity, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-266

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and having completely passed beyond the base of infinite consciousness, accompanied by the perception of the base of nothingness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by equanimity, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-267

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and having completely passed beyond the base of nothingness, accompanied by the perception of the base of neither perception nor non-perception, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, accompanied by equanimity, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The four immaterial absorptions, sixteenfold.
The wholesome mind of the immaterial sphere. ^1-268

#### The Wholesome Mind of the Three Planes ^1-1-4-0

##### The Wholesome Mind of the Sense Sphere ^1-1-4-1-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge, inferior… middling… superior… with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with inquiry as the dominant factor… with desire as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with energy as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with mind as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with inquiry as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-269

What are wholesome states? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense sphere has arisen, accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge… accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge… accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge… accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting, inferior… middling… superior… with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with desire as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with energy as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with mind as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The wholesome mind of the sense sphere. ^1-270

##### The Wholesome Mind of the Fine-Material Sphere ^1-1-4-2-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, using earth as the meditation device, inferior… middling… superior… with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with inquiry as the dominant factor… with desire as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with energy as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with mind as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with inquiry as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-271

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, using earth as the meditation device, inferior… middling… superior… with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with inquiry as the dominant factor… with desire as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with energy as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with mind as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior… with inquiry as the dominant factor, inferior… middling… superior, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The wholesome mind of the fine-material sphere. ^1-272

What are wholesome states? #### The World-Transcending Wholesome Mind ^1-1-5-0

##### Plain Progress ^1-1-5-1-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of faith, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of mindfulness, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of wisdom, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is the faculty of coming to know what is not yet known, there is right view, there is right thought, there is right speech, there is right action, there is right livelihood, there is right effort, there is right mindfulness, there is right concentration, there is the power of faith, there is the power of energy, there is the power of mindfulness, there is the power of concentration, there is the power of wisdom, there is the power of moral shame, there is the power of fear of wrongdoing, there is non-greed, there is non-hate, there is non-delusion, there is non-covetousness, there is non-ill-will, there is right view, there is moral shame, there is fear of wrongdoing, there is tranquillity of the mental body, there is tranquillity of mind, there is lightness of the mental body, there is lightness of mind, there is pliancy of the mental body, there is pliancy of mind, there is workability of the mental body, there is workability of mind, there is proficiency of the mental body, there is proficiency of mind, there is uprightness of the mental body, there is uprightness of mind, there is mindfulness, there is clear awareness, there is calm, there is insight, there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states. ^1-277

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-278

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is feeling at that time. ^1-279

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-280

What is intention at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is intention at that time. ^1-281

What is mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is mind at that time. ^1-282

What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, right thought, a path factor, included in the path – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-283

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-284

What is rapture at that time? Whatever at that time is rapture, gladness, delight, rejoicing, mirth, merriment, satisfaction, elation, contentment of mind, the awakening factor of rapture – this is rapture at that time. ^1-285

What is happiness at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is happiness at that time. ^1-286

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration, the awakening factor of concentration, a path factor, included in the path – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-287

What is the faculty of faith at that time? Whatever at that time is faith, trusting, confiding, full confidence, faith, the faculty of faith, the power of faith – this is the faculty of faith at that time. ^1-288

What is the faculty of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort, the awakening factor of energy, a path factor, included in the path – this is the faculty of energy at that time. ^1-289

What is the faculty of mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness, the awakening factor of mindfulness, a path factor, included in the path – this is the faculty of mindfulness at that time. ^1-290

What is the faculty of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration, the awakening factor of concentration, a path factor, included in the path – this is the faculty of concentration at that time. ^1-291

What is the faculty of wisdom at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is the faculty of wisdom at that time. ^1-292

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-293

What is the faculty of joy at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the faculty of joy at that time. ^1-294

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time. ^1-295

What is the faculty of coming to know what is not yet known at that time? Whatever, for the realization of those states not yet known, not yet seen, not yet reached, not yet discovered and not yet realized, is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is the faculty of coming to know what is not yet known at that time. ^1-296

What is right view at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is right view at that time. ^1-297

What is right thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, right thought, a path factor, included in the path – this is right thought at that time. ^1-298

What is right speech at that time? Whatever at that time is avoidance of the four kinds of verbal misconduct, abstaining, refraining, abstinence, not doing, not performing, not committing, not overstepping the boundary, destroying the causeway, right speech, a path factor, included in the path – this is right speech at that time. ^1-299

What is right action at that time? Whatever at that time is avoidance of the three kinds of bodily misconduct, abstaining, refraining, abstinence, not doing, not performing, not committing, not overstepping the boundary, destroying the causeway, right action, a path factor, included in the path – this is right action at that time. ^1-300

What is right livelihood at that time? Whatever at that time is avoidance of wrong livelihood, abstaining, refraining, abstinence, not doing, not performing, not committing, not overstepping the boundary, destroying the causeway, right livelihood, a path factor, included in the path – this is right livelihood at that time. ^1-301

What is right effort at that time? What is right mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness, the awakening factor of mindfulness, a path factor, included in the path – this is right mindfulness at that time. ^1-303

What is right concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration, the awakening factor of concentration, a path factor, included in the path – this is right concentration at that time. ^1-304

What is the power of faith at that time? Whatever at that time is faith, trusting, confiding, full confidence, faith, the faculty of faith, the power of faith – this is the power of faith at that time. ^1-305

What is the power of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort, the awakening factor of energy, a path factor, included in the path – this is the power of energy at that time. ^1-306

What is the power of mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness, the awakening factor of mindfulness, a path factor, included in the path – this is the power of mindfulness at that time. ^1-307

What is the power of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration, the awakening factor of concentration, a path factor, included in the path – this is the power of concentration at that time. ^1-308

What is the power of wisdom at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is the power of wisdom at that time. ^1-309

What is the power of moral shame at that time? At that time, being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of moral shame at that time. ^1-310

What is the power of fear of wrongdoing at that time? At that time, fearing what one should fear, fearing engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of fear of wrongdoing at that time. ^1-311

What is non-greed at that time? Whatever at that time is non-greed, not being greedy, the state of not being greedy, non-lust, not lusting, the state of not lusting, non-covetousness, non-greed, the wholesome root – this is non-greed at that time. ^1-312

What is non-hate at that time? Whatever at that time is non-hate, not hating, the state of not hating, non-ill-will, absence of malice, non-hate, the wholesome root – this is non-hate at that time. ^1-313

What is non-delusion at that time? What is non-covetousness at that time? Whatever at that time is non-greed, not being greedy, the state of not being greedy, non-lust, not lusting, the state of not lusting, non-covetousness, non-greed, the wholesome root – this is non-covetousness at that time. ^1-315

What is moral shame at that time? At that time, being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is moral shame at that time. ^1-318

What is tranquillity of the mental body at that time? What is tranquillity of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is tranquillity, full tranquillity, calming, full calming, the state of being fully calmed, the awakening factor of tranquillity – this is tranquillity of mind at that time. ^1-321

What is lightness of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is lightness, quickness to change, freedom from slowness, freedom from sluggishness – this is lightness of the mental body at that time. ^1-322

What is lightness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is lightness, quickness to change, freedom from slowness, freedom from sluggishness – this is lightness of mind at that time. ^1-323

What is pliancy of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is pliancy, softness, freedom from hardness, freedom from rigidity – this is pliancy of the mental body at that time. ^1-324

What is pliancy of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is pliancy, softness, freedom from hardness, freedom from rigidity – this is pliancy of mind at that time. ^1-325

What is workability of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is workability, being workable, the condition of being workable – this is workability of the mental body at that time. ^1-326

What is workability of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is workability, being workable, the condition of being workable – this is workability of mind at that time. ^1-327

What is proficiency of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is proficiency, being proficient, the condition of being proficient – this is proficiency of the mental body at that time. ^1-328

What is proficiency of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is proficiency, being proficient, the condition of being proficient – this is proficiency of mind at that time. ^1-329

What is uprightness of the mental body at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception and the aggregate of mental formations, is straightness, uprightness, freedom from deviousness, freedom from crookedness, freedom from twistedness – this is uprightness of the mental body at that time. ^1-330

What is uprightness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time, in the aggregate of consciousness, is straightness, uprightness, freedom from deviousness, freedom from crookedness, freedom from twistedness – this is uprightness of mind at that time. ^1-331

What is mindfulness at that time? Whatever at that time is mindfulness, recollection, recall, mindfulness, remembering, keeping in mind, not floating away, not forgetting, mindfulness, the faculty of mindfulness, the power of mindfulness, right mindfulness, the awakening factor of mindfulness, a path factor, included in the path – this is mindfulness at that time. ^1-332

What is clear awareness at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is clear awareness at that time. ^1-333

What is calm at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration, the awakening factor of concentration, a path factor, included in the path – this is calm at that time. ^1-334

What is insight at that time? Whatever at that time is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is insight at that time. ^1-335

What is exertion at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, right effort, the awakening factor of energy, a path factor, included in the path – this is exertion at that time. ^1-336

What is non-distraction at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, right concentration, the awakening factor of concentration, a path factor, included in the path – this is non-distraction at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are nine faculties, there is a five-factored absorption, there is an eight-factored path, there are seven powers, there are three roots, there is one contact, there is one feeling, there is one perception, there is one intention, there is one mind, there is one aggregate of feeling, there is one aggregate of perception, there is one aggregate of mental formations, there is one aggregate of consciousness, there is one mind base, there is one faculty of mind, there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states…. ^1-337

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of faith, the faculty of energy, the faculty of mindfulness, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of wisdom, the faculty of life, the faculty of coming to know what is not yet known, right view, right thought, right speech, right action, right livelihood, right effort, right mindfulness, right concentration, the power of faith, the power of energy, the power of mindfulness, the power of concentration, the power of wisdom, the power of moral shame, the power of fear of wrongdoing, non-greed, non-hate, non-delusion, non-covetousness, non-ill-will, right view, moral shame, fear of wrongdoing, tranquillity of the mental body, tranquillity of mind, lightness of the mental body, lightness of mind, pliancy of the mental body, pliancy of mind, workability of the mental body, workability of mind, proficiency of the mental body, proficiency of mind, uprightness of the mental body, uprightness of mind, mindfulness, clear awareness, calm, insight, exertion, non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are wholesome states. ^1-338

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and quick direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-339

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with pleasant progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-340

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with pleasant progress and quick direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-341

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge… with painful progress and quick direct knowledge… with pleasant progress and slow direct knowledge… with pleasant progress and quick direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
Plain progress. ^1-342

##### The Twenty Great Methods ^1-1-5-6-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops a world-transcending path… develops a world-transcending foundation of mindfulness… develops a world-transcending right striving… develops a world-transcending basis of spiritual power… develops a world-transcending faculty… develops a world-transcending power… develops a world-transcending awakening factor… develops a world-transcending truth… develops world-transcending calm… develops a world-transcending state… develops a world-transcending aggregate… develops a world-transcending sense base… develops a world-transcending element… develops a world-transcending nutriment… develops world-transcending contact… develops world-transcending feeling… develops world-transcending perception… develops world-transcending intention… develops a world-transcending mind, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The twenty great methods. ^1-357

##### The Dominant Factors ^1-1-5-7-0

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with inquiry as the dominant factor, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-358

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with inquiry as the dominant factor, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. ^1-359

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops a world-transcending path… develops a world-transcending foundation of mindfulness… develops a world-transcending right striving… develops a world-transcending basis of spiritual power… develops a world-transcending faculty… develops a world-transcending power… develops a world-transcending awakening factor… develops a world-transcending truth… develops world-transcending calm… develops a world-transcending state… develops a world-transcending aggregate… develops a world-transcending sense base… develops a world-transcending element… develops a world-transcending nutriment… develops world-transcending contact… develops world-transcending feeling… develops world-transcending perception… develops world-transcending intention… develops a world-transcending mind, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, with desire as the dominant factor… with energy as the dominant factor… with mind as the dominant factor… with inquiry as the dominant factor, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The dominant factors.
The first path. ^1-360

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the weakening of sensual lust and ill will, for reaching the second stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is the faculty of final knowledge… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The second path. ^1-361

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning without remainder of sensual lust and ill will, for reaching the third stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is the faculty of final knowledge… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states.
The third path. ^1-362

What are wholesome states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning without remainder of lust for fine-material existence, lust for immaterial existence, conceit, restlessness and ignorance, for reaching the fourth stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is the faculty of final knowledge… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states…. ^1-363

What is the faculty of final knowledge at that time? Whatever, for the realization of those states known, seen, reached, discovered and realized, is wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path, this is the faculty of final knowledge at that time… there is non-distraction… or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are wholesome states.
The fourth path.
The world-transcending mind. ^1-364

#### The Twelve Unwholesome Minds ^1-1-6-0

What are unwholesome states? At a time when an unwholesome mind has arisen, accompanied by joy and associated with wrong view, with a visible form as its object, or a sound as its object, or a smell as its object, or a taste as its object, or a touch as its object, or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life, there is wrong view, there is wrong thought, there is wrong effort, there is wrong concentration, there is the power of energy, there is the power of concentration, there is the power of shamelessness, there is the power of fearlessness of wrongdoing, there is greed, there is delusion, there is covetousness, there is wrong view, there is shamelessness, there is fearlessness of wrongdoing, there is calm, there is exertion, there is non-distraction; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are unwholesome states. ^1-365

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-366

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is feeling at that time. ^1-367

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-368

What is intention at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is intention at that time. ^1-369

What is mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is mind at that time. ^1-370

What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, wrong thought – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-371

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-372

What is rapture at that time? Whatever at that time is rapture, gladness, delight, rejoicing, mirth, merriment, satisfaction, elation, contentment of mind – this is rapture at that time. ^1-373

What is happiness at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is happiness at that time. ^1-374

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, wrong concentration – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-375

What is the faculty of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, wrong effort – this is the faculty of energy at that time. ^1-376

What is the faculty of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, wrong concentration – this is the faculty of concentration at that time? ^1-377

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-378

What is the faculty of joy at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the faculty of joy at that time. ^1-379

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time. ^1-380

What is wrong view at that time? Whatever at that time is view, holding views, the thicket of views, the wilderness of views, the contortion of views, the vacillation of views, the fetter of views, grasping, fixation [variant: paṭiggāho (Sī., Syā.)], adherence, misapprehension, a wrong road, a wrong path, wrongness, a basis for sectarian doctrines, a distorted grasp [variant: vipariyesagāho (Ka.)] – this is wrong view at that time. ^1-381

What is wrong thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind, wrong thought – this is wrong thought at that time. ^1-382

What is wrong effort at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, wrong effort – this is wrong effort at that time. ^1-383

What is wrong concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, wrong concentration – this is wrong concentration at that time. ^1-384

What is the power of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, wrong effort – this is the power of energy at that time. ^1-385

What is the power of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, wrong concentration – this is the power of concentration at that time. ^1-386

What is the power of shamelessness at that time? At that time, not being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, not being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of shamelessness at that time. ^1-387

What is the power of fearlessness of wrongdoing at that time? At that time, not fearing what one should fear, not fearing engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is the power of fearlessness of wrongdoing at that time. ^1-388

What is greed at that time? Whatever at that time is greed, being greedy, the state of being greedy, lust, lusting, the state of lusting, covetousness, greed, the unwholesome root – this is greed at that time. ^1-389

What is delusion at that time? Whatever at that time is lack of knowledge, lack of vision, non-realisation, lack of understanding, lack of awakening, non-penetration, failure to take in, failure to fathom, failure to consider, failure to review, failure to see for oneself, stupidity, foolishness, lack of clear awareness, delusion, deep delusion, utter delusion, ignorance, the flood of ignorance, the yoke of ignorance, the underlying tendency to ignorance, the obsession of ignorance, the bar of ignorance, delusion, the unwholesome root – this is delusion at that time. ^1-390

What is covetousness at that time? Whatever at that time is greed, being greedy, the state of being greedy, lust, lusting, the state of lusting, covetousness, greed, the unwholesome root – this is covetousness at that time. ^1-391

What is wrong view at that time? Whatever at that time is view, holding views, the thicket of views, the wilderness of views, the contortion of views, the vacillation of views, the fetter of views, grasping, fixation, adherence, misapprehension, a wrong road, a wrong path, wrongness, a basis for sectarian doctrines, a distorted grasp – this is wrong view at that time. ^1-392

What is shamelessness at that time? At that time, not being ashamed of what one should be ashamed of, not being ashamed of engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is shamelessness at that time. ^1-393

What is fearlessness of wrongdoing at that time? At that time, not fearing what one should fear, not fearing engaging in bad, unwholesome states – this is fearlessness of wrongdoing at that time. ^1-394

What is calm at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, wrong concentration – this is calm at that time. ^1-395

What is exertion at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy, wrong effort – this is exertion at that time. ^1-396

What is non-distraction at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration, wrong concentration – this is non-distraction at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are unwholesome states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are five faculties, there is a five-factored absorption, there is a four-factored path, there are four powers, there are two roots, there is one contact… there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are unwholesome states…. ^1-397

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? #### Indeterminate Results ^1-1-7-0

##### The Five Kinds of Consciousness Resulting from Wholesome Action ^1-1-7-1-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of wholesome action of the sense sphere that has been done and accumulated, eye-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-431

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-432

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding eye-consciousness element, is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is feeling at that time. ^1-433

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding eye-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-434

What is intention at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding eye-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is intention at that time. ^1-435

What is mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding eye-consciousness element – this is mind at that time. ^1-436

What is equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is equanimity at that time. ^1-437

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-438

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding eye-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-439

What is the faculty of equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is the faculty of equanimity at that time. ^1-440

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one eye-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-441

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states. ^1-442

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of wholesome action of the sense sphere that has been done and accumulated, ear-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a sound as its object… nose-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a smell as its object… tongue-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a taste as its object… body-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by happiness, with a touch as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of pleasure, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-443

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-444

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding body-consciousness element, is bodily comfort, bodily pleasure, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of body-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of body-contact – this is feeling at that time. ^1-445

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding body-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-446

What is intention at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding body-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is intention at that time. ^1-447

What is happiness at that time? Whatever at that time is bodily comfort, bodily pleasure, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of body-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of body-contact – this is happiness at that time. ^1-449

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-450

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding body-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-451

What is the faculty of pleasure at that time? Whatever at that time is bodily comfort, bodily pleasure, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of body-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of body-contact – this is the faculty of pleasure at that time. ^1-452

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one body-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-453

Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.

What are indeterminate states? What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-461

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-462

What is equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is equanimity at that time. ^1-463

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-464

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-465

What is the faculty of equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is the faculty of equanimity at that time. ^1-466

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-467
