Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The mind element resulting from wholesome action. ^1-468

##### The Mind-Consciousness Element Resulting from Wholesome Action, Accompanied by Joy ^1-1-7-3-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of wholesome action of the sense sphere that has been done and accumulated, the mind-consciousness element has arisen, accompanied by joy, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-469

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-470

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is feeling at that time. ^1-471

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-472

What is intention at that time. Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is intention at that time. ^1-473

What is mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is mind at that time. ^1-474

What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-475

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-476

What is rapture at that time? Whatever at that time is rapture, gladness, delight, rejoicing, mirth, merriment, satisfaction, elation, contentment of mind – this is rapture at that time. ^1-477

What is happiness at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is happiness at that time. ^1-478

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-479

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-480

What is the faculty of joy at that time? Whatever at that time is mental comfort, mental happiness, the comfortable, pleasant experience born of mind-contact, the comfortable, pleasant feeling born of mind-contact – this is the faculty of joy at that time. ^1-481

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-482

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The mind-consciousness element resulting from wholesome action, accompanied by joy. ^1-483

##### The Mind-Consciousness Element Resulting from Wholesome Action, Accompanied by Equanimity ^1-1-7-4-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of wholesome action of the sense sphere that has been done and accumulated, the mind-consciousness element has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-484

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-485

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is feeling at that time. ^1-486

What is perception at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is perception, perceiving, the state of having perceived – this is perception at that time. ^1-487

What is intention at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding mind-consciousness element, is intention, intending, the state of having intended – this is intention at that time. ^1-488

What is mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is mind at that time. ^1-489

What is applied thought at that time? Whatever at that time is thinking, applied thought, thought, fixing, firm fixing, the directing of the mind – this is applied thought at that time. ^1-490

What is sustained thought at that time? Whatever at that time is exploring, sustained thought, continued exploring, close exploring, the mind's staying connected, its continued observing – this is sustained thought at that time. ^1-491

What is equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is equanimity at that time. ^1-492

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-493

What is the faculty of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is mind, intellect, mental activity, heart, radiance, intellect, the mind base, the faculty of mind, consciousness, the aggregate of consciousness, the corresponding mind-consciousness element – this is the faculty of mind at that time. ^1-494

What is the faculty of equanimity at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental state that is neither comfortable nor uncomfortable, the experience born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant, the feeling born of mind-contact that is neither painful nor pleasant – this is the faculty of equanimity at that time. ^1-495

What is the faculty of life at that time? Whatever, in those immaterial states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the faculty of life at that time; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-496

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The mind-consciousness element resulting from wholesome action, accompanied by equanimity. ^1-497

##### The Eight Great Results ^1-1-7-5-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of wholesome action of the sense sphere that has been done and accumulated, the mind-consciousness element has arisen, accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge… accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge… accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge… accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge… accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states… non-greed, the indeterminate root… non-hate, the indeterminate root… these are indeterminate states.
The eight great results. ^1-498

##### Results of the Fine-Material Sphere ^1-1-7-6-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same wholesome action of the fine-material sphere that has been done and accumulated, when one, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-499

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the fine-material sphere and with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same wholesome action of the fine-material sphere that has been done and accumulated, when one, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states.
Results of the fine-material sphere. ^1-500

##### Results of the Immaterial Sphere ^1-1-7-7-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and by completely passing beyond perceptions of material form, by the disappearance of perceptions of impact, by not attending to perceptions of diversity, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite space, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same wholesome action of the immaterial sphere that has been done and accumulated, when one, by completely passing beyond perceptions of material form, by the disappearance of perceptions of impact, by not attending to perceptions of diversity, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite space, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-501

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and having completely passed beyond the base of infinite space, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite consciousness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same wholesome action of the immaterial sphere that has been done and accumulated, when one, having completely passed beyond the base of infinite space, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite consciousness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-502

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and having completely passed beyond the base of infinite consciousness, accompanied by the perception of the base of nothingness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same wholesome action of the immaterial sphere that has been done and accumulated, when one, having completely passed beyond the base of infinite consciousness, accompanied by the perception of the base of nothingness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-503

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the path to rebirth in the immaterial sphere and having completely passed beyond the base of nothingness, accompanied by the perception of the base of neither perception nor non-perception, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same wholesome action of the immaterial sphere that has been done and accumulated, when one, having completely passed beyond the base of nothingness, accompanied by the perception of the base of neither perception nor non-perception, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states.
Results of the immaterial sphere. ^1-504

As a result of that same world-transcending wholesome absorption that has been done and developed, when one, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, which is empty, at that time there is contact… there is the faculty of final knowledge… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-505

##### The Twenty Great Methods ^1-1-8-6-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops the world-transcending path… develops a world-transcending foundation of mindfulness… develops a world-transcending right striving… develops a world-transcending basis of spiritual power… develops a world-transcending faculty… develops a world-transcending power… develops a world-transcending awakening factor… develops a world-transcending truth… develops a world-transcending calm… develops a world-transcending state… develops a world-transcending aggregate… develops a world-transcending sense base… develops a world-transcending element… develops a world-transcending nutriment… develops a world-transcending contact… develops a world-transcending feeling… develops a world-transcending perception… develops a world-transcending intention… develops a world-transcending mind, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states; because that same world-transcending wholesome absorption has been done and developed, as its result one, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, which is empty… which is signless… which is desireless, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states.
The twenty great methods. ^1-528

##### Desire as the Dominant Factor: Plain Progress ^1-1-8-7-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the abandoning of wrong views, for reaching the first stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, with desire as the dominant factor, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same world-transcending wholesome absorption that has been done and developed, when one, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, which is empty, with desire as the dominant factor, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-529

The result of the first path. ^1-552

##### The Result of the Second and Later Paths ^1-1-8-9-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops world-transcending absorption, which leads to liberation and leads away from accumulation, for the weakening of sensual lust and ill will, for reaching the second stage… for the abandoning without remainder of sensual lust and ill will, for reaching the third stage… for the abandoning without remainder of lust for fine-material existence, lust for immaterial existence, conceit, restlessness and ignorance, for reaching the fourth stage, and quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, at that time there is contact… there is the faculty of final knowledge… there is non-distraction… these are wholesome states. As a result of that same world-transcending wholesome absorption that has been done and developed, when one, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, with painful progress and slow direct knowledge, which is empty, at that time there is contact… there is the faculty of one who has final knowledge… there is non-distraction… or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-553

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time…. ^1-554

What is the faculty of one who has final knowledge at that time? Whatever, in those states of one who has final knowledge, is final knowledge, wisdom, understanding, investigation, close investigation, investigation of states, discerning, distinguishing, differentiating, learning, skill, subtlety, clarity, consideration, examination, breadth, intelligence, guidance, insight, clear awareness, a goad, wisdom, the faculty of wisdom, the power of wisdom, the sword of wisdom, the tower of wisdom, the light of wisdom, the radiance of wisdom, the lamp of wisdom, the jewel of wisdom, non-delusion, investigation of states, right view, the awakening factor of investigation of states, a path factor, included in the path – this is the faculty of one who has final knowledge at that time… or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
The result of the second and later paths.
The world-transcending result. ^1-555

#### Indeterminate Results of the Unwholesome ^1-1-9-0

##### The Five Kinds of Consciousness Resulting from the Unwholesome ^1-1-9-1-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of unwholesome action that has been done and accumulated, eye-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object… ear-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a sound as its object… nose-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a smell as its object… tongue-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a taste as its object… body-consciousness has arisen, accompanied by pain, with a touch as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is pain, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of pain, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-556

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time. ^1-557

What is feeling at that time? Whatever at that time, born of the contact of the corresponding body-consciousness element, is bodily discomfort, bodily pain, the uncomfortable, painful experience born of body-contact, the uncomfortable, painful feeling born of body-contact – this is feeling at that time…. ^1-558

What is pain at that time? Whatever at that time is bodily discomfort, bodily pain, the uncomfortable, painful experience born of body-contact, the uncomfortable, painful feeling born of body-contact – this is pain at that time…. ^1-559

What is the faculty of pain at that time? Whatever at that time is bodily discomfort, bodily pain, the uncomfortable, painful experience born of body-contact, the uncomfortable, painful feeling born of body-contact – this is the faculty of pain at that time… or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one body-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-560

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The five kinds of consciousness resulting from the unwholesome. ^1-561

##### The Mind Element Resulting from the Unwholesome ^1-1-9-2-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of unwholesome action that has been done and accumulated, the mind element has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object… or a touch as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-562

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The mind element resulting from the unwholesome. ^1-563

##### The Mind-Consciousness Element Resulting from the Unwholesome ^1-1-9-3-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when, as a result of unwholesome action that has been done and accumulated, the mind-consciousness element has arisen, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-564

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The mind-consciousness element resulting from the unwholesome.
Indeterminate results. ^1-565

#### Functional Indeterminate Minds without Roots ^1-1-10-0

##### The Functional Mind Element ^1-1-10-1-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when the mind element has arisen, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object… or a touch as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are three faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-566

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The functional mind element. ^1-567

##### The Functional Mind-Consciousness Element Accompanied by Joy ^1-1-10-2-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when the mind-consciousness element has arisen, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, accompanied by joy, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is rapture, there is happiness, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of joy, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states. ^1-568

What is contact at that time? Whatever at that time is contact, touching, coming into contact, the state of having come into contact – this is contact at that time…. ^1-569

What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration – this is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-570

What is the faculty of energy at that time? Whatever at that time is the mental arousing of energy, setting out, pressing forward, striving, effort, zeal, ardour, strength, steadfastness, unslackening pressing forward, not putting down desire, not putting down the burden, firm grasping of the burden, energy, the faculty of energy, the power of energy – this is the faculty of energy at that time. ^1-571

What is the faculty of concentration at that time? Whatever at that time is the stability of mind, its steadiness, its firmness, non-scattering, non-distraction, the state of an unscattered mind, calm, the faculty of concentration, the power of concentration – this is the faculty of concentration at that time… or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states.
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are five faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-572

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, rapture, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of energy, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The functional mind-consciousness element accompanied by joy. ^1-573

##### The Functional Mind-Consciousness Element Accompanied by Equanimity ^1-1-10-3-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when the mind-consciousness element has arisen, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, accompanied by equanimity, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact, there is feeling, there is perception, there is intention, there is mind, there is applied thought, there is sustained thought, there is equanimity, there is one-pointedness of mind, there is the faculty of energy, there is the faculty of concentration, there is the faculty of mind, there is the faculty of equanimity, there is the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states….
Now at that time there are four aggregates, there are two sense bases, there are two elements, there are three nutriments, there are five faculties, there is one contact… there is one mind-consciousness element, there is one mental-object base, there is one mental-object element; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time – these are indeterminate states…. ^1-574

What is the aggregate of mental formations at that time? Contact, intention, applied thought, sustained thought, one-pointedness of mind, the faculty of energy, the faculty of concentration, the faculty of life; or whatever other dependently arisen immaterial states there are at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness – this is the aggregate of mental formations at that time… these are indeterminate states.
The functional mind-consciousness element accompanied by equanimity.
Functional indeterminate minds without roots. ^1-575

##### Functional Minds of the Sense Sphere with Roots ^1-1-10-4-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when the mind-consciousness element has arisen, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge… accompanied by joy and associated with knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge… accompanied by joy and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge… accompanied by equanimity and associated with knowledge, with prompting… accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge… accompanied by equanimity and dissociated from knowledge, with prompting, with a visible form as its object… or a mental object as its object, or whatever it takes up as its object, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states… non-greed, the indeterminate root… non-hate, the indeterminate root… these are indeterminate states.
Functional minds of the sense sphere with roots. ^1-576

##### Functional Minds of the Fine-Material Sphere ^1-1-10-5-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops absorption of the fine-material sphere, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, for a pleasant abiding here and now, and, quite secluded from sensual pleasures… enters and dwells in the first absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-577

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops absorption of the fine-material sphere, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, for a pleasant abiding here and now, and, with the stilling of applied thought and sustained thought… the second absorption… the third absorption… the fourth absorption… the first absorption… enters and dwells in the fifth absorption, using earth as the meditation device, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states.
Functional minds of the fine-material sphere. ^1-578

##### Functional Minds of the Immaterial Sphere ^1-1-10-6-0

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops absorption of the immaterial sphere, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, for a pleasant abiding here and now, and, by completely passing beyond perceptions of material form, by the disappearance of perceptions of impact, by not attending to perceptions of diversity, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite space, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-579

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops absorption of the immaterial sphere, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, for a pleasant abiding here and now, and, having completely passed beyond the base of infinite space, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite consciousness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-580

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops absorption of the immaterial sphere, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, for a pleasant abiding here and now, and, having completely passed beyond the base of infinite consciousness, accompanied by the perception of the base of nothingness, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states. ^1-581

What are indeterminate states? At a time when one develops absorption of the immaterial sphere, functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor a result of action, for a pleasant abiding here and now, and, having completely passed beyond the base of nothingness, accompanied by the perception of the base of neither perception nor non-perception, with the abandoning of pleasure… enters and dwells in the fourth absorption, at that time there is contact… there is non-distraction… these are indeterminate states… non-greed, the indeterminate root… non-hate, the indeterminate root… non-delusion, the indeterminate root… these are indeterminate states.
Functional minds of the immaterial sphere.
Functional indeterminate minds.
The chapter on the arising of mind is complete. ^1-582

### The Chapter on Material Form ^1-2-0

#### The Opening Statement ^1-2-1-0

What are indeterminate states? The results of wholesome and unwholesome states, of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere, of the immaterial sphere, and not included in the spheres of existence: the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, the aggregate of consciousness; the states that are functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor results of action; all material form; and the unconditioned element – these are indeterminate states. ^1-583

#### The Matrix ^1-2-2-0

##### The Sets of One ^1-2-2-1-0

Therein, what is all material form? The four great elements and the material form derived from the four great elements – this is called all material form. All material form is not a root, is without roots, is dissociated from roots, has conditions, is conditioned, is material [variant: rūpiyaṃ (Sī.)], is worldly, is subject to taints, is subject to fetters, is subject to ties, is subject to floods, is subject to yokes, is subject to hindrances, is misapprehended, is subject to clinging, is subject to defilement, is indeterminate, is without an object, is not a mental factor, is dissociated from mind, neither is a result nor produces results, is undefiled but subject to defilement, is not with applied thought and sustained thought, is not without applied thought but with sustained thought only, is without applied thought and without sustained thought, is not accompanied by rapture, is not accompanied by happiness, is not accompanied by equanimity, is to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development, is among the states whose roots are to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development, leads neither to nor away from accumulation, is of one neither in training nor beyond training, is limited, is of the sense sphere, is not of the fine-material sphere, is not of the immaterial sphere, is included in the spheres of existence, is not among the states not included in the spheres of existence, is not fixed, does not lead to liberation, is arisen, is knowable by the six kinds of consciousness, is impermanent, is overcome by ageing.
Thus material form is classified in one way.
The sets of one. ^1-584

##### The Pairs ^1-2-2-2-0

Material form classified in two ways –
There is material form that is derived; there is material form that is not derived.
There is material form that is produced by past action; there is material form that is not produced by past action.
There is material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging; there is material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging.
There is material form that is visible; there is material form that is invisible.
There is material form that makes impact; there is material form that makes no impact.
There is material form that is a faculty; there is material form that is not a faculty.
There is material form that is a great element; there is material form that is not a great element.
There is material form that is intimation; there is material form that is not intimation.
There is material form arising from mind; there is material form not arising from mind.
There is material form existing together with mind; there is material form not existing together with mind.
There is material form accompanying mind; there is material form not accompanying mind.
There is material form that is internal; there is material form that is external.
There is material form that is gross; there is material form that is subtle.
There is material form that is far; there is material form that is near.
There is material form that is a basis for eye-contact; there is material form that is not a basis for eye-contact. There is material form that is a basis for feeling born of eye-contact… for perception… for intention… for eye-consciousness; there is material form that is not a basis for eye-consciousness.
There is material form that is a basis for ear-contact… for nose-contact… for tongue-contact… for body-contact; there is material form that is not a basis for body-contact. There is material form that is a basis for feeling born of body-contact… for perception… for intention… for body-consciousness; there is material form that is not a basis for body-consciousness.
There is material form that is an object of eye-contact; there is material form that is not an object of eye-contact. There is material form that is an object of feeling born of eye-contact… of perception… of intention… of eye-consciousness; there is material form that is not an object of eye-consciousness.
There is material form that is an object of ear-contact… of nose-contact… of tongue-contact… of body-contact; there is material form that is not an object of body-contact. There is material form that is an object of feeling born of body-contact… of perception… of intention… of body-consciousness; there is material form that is not an object of body-consciousness.
There is material form that is the eye base; there is material form that is not the eye base. There is material form that is the ear base… the nose base… the tongue base… the body base; there is material form that is not the body base.
There is material form that is the visible-form base; there is material form that is not the visible-form base. There is material form that is the sound base… the smell base… the taste base… the touch base; there is material form that is not the touch base.
There is material form that is the eye element; there is material form that is not the eye element. There is material form that is the ear element… the nose element… the tongue element… the body element; there is material form that is not the body element.
There is material form that is the visible-form element; there is material form that is not the visible-form element. There is material form that is the sound element… the smell element… the taste element… the touch element; there is material form that is not the touch element.
There is material form that is the faculty of the eye; there is material form that is not the faculty of the eye. There is material form that is the faculty of the ear… the faculty of the nose… the faculty of the tongue… the faculty of the body; there is material form that is not the faculty of the body.
There is material form that is the faculty of femininity; there is material form that is not the faculty of femininity.
There is material form that is the faculty of masculinity; there is material form that is not the faculty of masculinity.
There is material form that is the faculty of life; there is material form that is not the faculty of life.
There is material form that is bodily intimation; there is material form that is not bodily intimation.
There is material form that is verbal intimation; there is material form that is not verbal intimation.
There is material form that is the space element; there is material form that is not the space element.
There is material form that is the water element; there is material form that is not the water element.
There is material form that is lightness of material form; there is material form that is not lightness of material form.
There is material form that is pliancy of material form; there is material form that is not pliancy of material form.
There is material form that is workability of material form; there is material form that is not workability of material form.
There is material form that is growth of material form; there is material form that is not growth of material form.
There is material form that is continuity of material form; there is material form that is not continuity of material form.
There is material form that is ageing of material form; there is material form that is not ageing of material form.
There is material form that is impermanence of material form; there is material form that is not impermanence of material form.
There is material form that is edible food; there is material form that is not edible food.
Thus material form is classified in two ways.
The pairs. ^1-U1

##### The Sets of Three ^1-2-2-3-0

Material form classified in three ways –
Material form that is internal is derived. Of material form that is external, some is derived and some is not derived.
Material form that is internal is produced by past action. Of material form that is external, some is produced by past action and some is not produced by past action.
Material form that is internal is produced by past action and subject to clinging. Of material form that is external, some is produced by past action and subject to clinging and some is not produced by past action but subject to clinging.
Material form that is internal is invisible. Of material form that is external, some is visible and some is invisible.
Material form that is internal makes impact. Of material form that is external, some makes impact and some makes no impact.
Material form that is internal is a faculty. Of material form that is external, some is a faculty and some is not a faculty.
Material form that is internal is not a great element. Of material form that is external, some is a great element and some is not a great element.
Material form that is internal is not intimation. Of material form that is external, some is intimation and some is not intimation.
Material form that is internal does not arise from mind. Of material form that is external, some arises from mind and some does not arise from mind.
Material form that is internal does not exist together with mind. Of material form that is external, some exists together with mind and some does not exist together with mind.
Material form that is internal does not accompany mind. Of material form that is external, some accompanies mind and some does not accompany mind.
Material form that is internal is gross. Of material form that is external, some is gross and some is subtle.
Material form that is internal is near. Of material form that is external, some is far and some is near.
Material form that is external is not a basis for eye-contact. Of material form that is internal, some is a basis for eye-contact and some is not a basis for eye-contact.
Material form that is external is not a basis for feeling born of eye-contact… for perception… for intention… for eye-consciousness. Of material form that is internal, some is a basis for eye-consciousness and some is not a basis for eye-consciousness.
Material form that is external is not a basis for ear-contact… for nose-contact… for tongue-contact… for body-contact. Of material form that is internal, some is a basis for body-contact and some is not a basis for body-contact. Material form that is external is not a basis for feeling born of body-contact… for perception… for intention… for body-consciousness. Of material form that is internal, some is a basis for body-consciousness and some is not a basis for body-consciousness.
Material form that is internal is not an object of eye-contact. Of material form that is external, some is an object of eye-contact and some is not an object of eye-contact. Material form that is internal is not an object of feeling born of eye-contact… of perception… of intention… of eye-consciousness. Of material form that is external, some is an object of eye-consciousness and some is not an object of eye-consciousness.
Material form that is internal is not an object of ear-contact… of nose-contact… of tongue-contact… of body-contact. Of material form that is external, some is an object of body-contact and some is not an object of body-contact. Material form that is internal is not an object of feeling born of body-contact… of perception… of intention… of body-consciousness. Of material form that is external, some is an object of body-consciousness and some is not an object of body-consciousness.
Material form that is external is not the eye base. Of material form that is internal, some is the eye base and some is not the eye base. Material form that is external is not the ear base… not the nose base… not the tongue base… not the body base. Of material form that is internal, some is the body base and some is not the body base.
Material form that is internal is not the visible-form base. Of material form that is external, some is the visible-form base and some is not the visible-form base. Material form that is internal is not the sound base… not the smell base… not the taste base… not the touch base. Of material form that is external, some is the touch base and some is not the touch base.
Material form that is external is not the eye element. Of material form that is internal, some is the eye element and some is not the eye element. Material form that is external is not the ear element… not the nose element… not the tongue element… not the body element. Of material form that is internal, some is the body element and some is not the body element.
Material form that is internal is not the visible-form element. Of material form that is external, some is the visible-form element and some is not the visible-form element. Material form that is internal is not the sound element… not the smell element… not the taste element… not the touch element. Of material form that is external, some is the touch element and some is not the touch element.
Material form that is external is not the faculty of the eye. Of material form that is internal, some is the faculty of the eye and some is not the faculty of the eye. Material form that is external is not the faculty of the ear… not the faculty of the nose… not the faculty of the tongue… not the faculty of the body. Of material form that is internal, some is the faculty of the body and some is not the faculty of the body.
Material form that is internal is not the faculty of femininity. Of material form that is external, some is the faculty of femininity and some is not the faculty of femininity.
Material form that is internal is not the faculty of masculinity. Of material form that is external, some is the faculty of masculinity and some is not the faculty of masculinity.
Material form that is internal is not the faculty of life. Of material form that is external, some is the faculty of life and some is not the faculty of life.
Material form that is internal is not bodily intimation. Of material form that is external, some is bodily intimation and some is not bodily intimation.
Material form that is internal is not verbal intimation. Of material form that is external, some is verbal intimation and some is not verbal intimation.
Material form that is internal is not the space element. Of material form that is external, some is the space element and some is not the space element.
Material form that is internal is not the water element. Of material form that is external, some is the water element and some is not the water element.
Material form that is internal is not lightness of material form. Of material form that is external, some is lightness of material form and some is not lightness of material form.
Material form that is internal is not pliancy of material form. Of material form that is external, some is pliancy of material form and some is not pliancy of material form.
Material form that is internal is not workability of material form. Of material form that is external, some is workability of material form and some is not workability of material form.
Material form that is internal is not growth of material form. Of material form that is external, some is growth of material form and some is not growth of material form.
Material form that is internal is not continuity of material form. Of material form that is external, some is continuity of material form and some is not continuity of material form.
Material form that is internal is not ageing of material form. Of material form that is external, some is ageing of material form and some is not ageing of material form.
Material form that is internal is not impermanence of material form. Of material form that is external, some is impermanence of material form and some is not impermanence of material form.
Material form that is internal is not edible food. Of material form that is external, some is edible food and some is not edible food.
Thus material form is classified in three ways.
The sets of three. ^1-585

##### The Sets of Four ^1-2-2-4-0

Material form classified in four ways –
Of material form that is derived, some is produced by past action and some is not produced by past action. Of material form that is not derived, some is produced by past action and some is not produced by past action.
Of material form that is derived, some is produced by past action and subject to clinging and some is not produced by past action but subject to clinging. Of material form that is not derived, some is produced by past action and subject to clinging and some is not produced by past action but subject to clinging.
Of material form that is derived, some makes impact and some makes no impact. Of material form that is not derived, some makes impact and some makes no impact.
Of material form that is derived, some is gross and some is subtle. Of material form that is not derived, some is gross and some is subtle.
Of material form that is derived, some is far and some is near. Of material form that is not derived, some is far and some is near.
Of material form that is produced by past action, some is visible and some is invisible. Of material form that is not produced by past action, some is visible and some is invisible.
Of material form that is produced by past action, some makes impact and some makes no impact. Of material form that is not produced by past action, some makes impact and some makes no impact.
Of material form that is produced by past action, some is a great element and some is not a great element. Of material form that is not produced by past action, some is a great element and some is not a great element.
Of material form that is produced by past action, some is gross and some is subtle. Of material form that is not produced by past action, some is gross and some is subtle.
Of material form that is produced by past action, some is far and some is near. Of material form that is not produced by past action, some is far and some is near.
Of material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, some is visible and some is invisible. Of material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, some is visible and some is invisible.
Of material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, some makes impact and some makes no impact. Of material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, some makes impact and some makes no impact.
Of material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, some is a great element and some is not a great element. Of material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, some is a great element and some is not a great element.
Of material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, some is gross and some is subtle. Of material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, some is gross and some is subtle.
Of material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, some is far and some is near. Of material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, some is far and some is near.
Of material form that makes impact, some is a faculty and some is not a faculty. Of material form that makes no impact, some is a faculty and some is not a faculty.
Of material form that makes impact, some is a great element and some is not a great element. Of material form that makes no impact, some is a great element and some is not a great element.
Of material form that is a faculty, some is gross and some is subtle. Of material form that is not a faculty, some is gross and some is subtle.
Of material form that is a faculty, some is far and some is near. Of material form that is not a faculty, some is far and some is near.
Of material form that is a great element, some is gross and some is subtle. Of material form that is not a great element, some is gross and some is subtle.
Of material form that is a great element, some is far and some is near. Of material form that is not a great element, some is far and some is near.
Material form that is seen, heard, sensed, cognized.
Thus material form is classified in four ways.
The sets of four. ^1-586

##### The Set of Five ^1-2-2-5-0

Material form classified in five ways –
The earth element, the water element, the fire element, the air element, and the material form that is derived.
Thus material form is classified in five ways.
The set of five. ^1-587

##### The Set of Six ^1-2-2-6-0

Material form classified in six ways –
Material form knowable by the eye, material form knowable by the ear, material form knowable by the nose, material form knowable by the tongue, material form knowable by the body, material form knowable by the mind.
Thus material form is classified in six ways.
The set of six. ^1-588

##### The Set of Seven ^1-2-2-7-0

Material form classified in seven ways –
Material form knowable by the eye, material form knowable by the ear, material form knowable by the nose, material form knowable by the tongue, material form knowable by the body, material form knowable by the mind element, material form knowable by the mind-consciousness element.
Thus material form is classified in seven ways.
The set of seven. ^1-589

##### The Set of Eight ^1-2-2-8-0

Material form classified in eight ways –
Material form knowable by the eye, material form knowable by the ear, material form knowable by the nose, material form knowable by the tongue, material form knowable by the body, some pleasant to touch, some painful to touch, material form knowable by the mind element, material form knowable by the mind-consciousness element.
Thus material form is classified in eight ways.
The set of eight. ^1-590

##### The Set of Nine ^1-2-2-9-0

Material form classified in nine ways –
The faculty of the eye, the faculty of the ear, the faculty of the nose, the faculty of the tongue, the faculty of the body, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, and the material form that is not a faculty.
Thus material form is classified in nine ways.
The set of nine. ^1-591

##### The Set of Ten ^1-2-2-10-0

Material form classified in ten ways –
The faculty of the eye, the faculty of the ear, the faculty of the nose, the faculty of the tongue, the faculty of the body, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, and material form that is not a faculty, of which some makes impact and some makes no impact.
Thus material form is classified in ten ways.
The set of ten. ^1-592

##### The Set of Eleven ^1-2-2-11-0

Material form classified in eleven ways –
The eye base, the ear base, the nose base, the tongue base, the body base, the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, and the material form that is invisible and makes no impact [variant: anidassanaṃ appaṭighaṃ (Sī., Syā.)] and is included in the mental-object base.
Thus material form is classified in eleven ways.
The set of eleven.
The matrix. ^1-593

#### The Classification of Material Form ^1-2-3-0

##### The Exposition of the Sets of One ^1-2-3-1-0

All material form is indeed not a root, is indeed without roots, is indeed dissociated from roots, indeed has conditions, is indeed conditioned, is indeed material, is indeed worldly, is indeed subject to taints, is indeed subject to fetters, is indeed subject to ties, is indeed subject to floods, is indeed subject to yokes, is indeed subject to hindrances, is indeed misapprehended, is indeed subject to clinging, is indeed subject to defilement, is indeed indeterminate, is indeed without an object, is indeed not a mental factor, is indeed dissociated from mind, indeed neither is a result nor produces results, is indeed undefiled but subject to defilement, is indeed not with applied thought and sustained thought, is indeed not without applied thought but with sustained thought only, is indeed without applied thought and without sustained thought, is indeed not accompanied by rapture, is indeed not accompanied by happiness, is indeed not accompanied by equanimity, is indeed to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development, is indeed among the states whose roots are to be abandoned neither by seeing nor by development, indeed leads neither to nor away from accumulation, is indeed of one neither in training nor beyond training, is indeed limited, is indeed of the sense sphere, is indeed not of the fine-material sphere, is indeed not of the immaterial sphere, is indeed included in the spheres of existence, is indeed not among the states not included in the spheres of existence, is indeed not fixed, indeed does not lead to liberation, is indeed knowable, once arisen, by the six kinds of consciousness, is indeed impermanent, is indeed overcome by ageing.
Thus material form is classified in one way.
The exposition of the sets of one. ^1-594

##### The Exposition of the Pairs ^1-2-3-2-0

###### The Analysis of Derived Material Form ^1-2-3-2-1-0

What is the material form that is derived? The eye base, the ear base, the nose base, the tongue base, the body base, the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, the space element, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, growth of material form, continuity of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, edible food. ^1-595

What is the material form that is the eye base? The eye [variant: cakkhuṃ (Sī., Syā.)] that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the eye, invisible and making impact, with which one has seen, sees, will see or might see a visible form that is visible and makes impact; this is the eye, this is the eye base, this is the eye element, this is the faculty of the eye, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is a guide, this is a leader, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the eye base. ^1-596

What is the material form that is the eye base? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the eye, invisible and making impact, on which a visible form that is visible and makes impact has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is the eye, this is the eye base, this is the eye element, this is the faculty of the eye, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is a guide, this is a leader, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the eye base. ^1-597

What is the material form that is the eye base? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the eye, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against a visible form that is visible and makes impact; this is the eye, this is the eye base, this is the eye element, this is the faculty of the eye, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is a guide, this is a leader, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the eye base. ^1-598

What is the material form that is the eye base? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the eye depending on which, on account of a visible form, eye-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the eye depending on which, on account of a visible form, feeling born of eye-contact… perception… intention… eye-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the eye depending on which eye-contact with a visible form as its object has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the eye depending on which feeling born of eye-contact with a visible form as its object… perception… intention… eye-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is the eye, this is the eye base, this is the eye element, this is the faculty of the eye, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is a guide, this is a leader, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the eye base. ^1-599

What is the material form that is the ear base? The ear that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the ear, invisible and making impact, with which one has heard, hears, will hear or might hear a sound that is invisible and makes impact; this is the ear, this is the ear base, this is the ear element, this is the faculty of the ear, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the ear base. ^1-600

What is the material form that is the ear base? The ear that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the ear, invisible and making impact, on which a sound that is invisible and makes impact has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is the ear, this is the ear base, this is the ear element, this is the faculty of the ear, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the ear base. ^1-601

What is the material form that is the ear base? The ear that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the ear, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against a sound that is invisible and makes impact; this is the ear, this is the ear base, this is the ear element, this is the faculty of the ear, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the ear base. ^1-602

What is the material form that is the ear base? The ear that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the ear depending on which, on account of a sound, ear-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the ear depending on which, on account of a sound, feeling born of ear-contact… perception… intention… ear-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the ear depending on which ear-contact with a sound as its object has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the ear depending on which feeling born of ear-contact with a sound as its object… perception… intention… ear-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is the ear, this is the ear base, this is the ear element, this is the faculty of the ear, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the ear base. ^1-603

What is the material form that is the nose base? The nose that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the nose, invisible and making impact, with which one has smelled, smells, will smell or might smell a smell that is invisible and makes impact; this is the nose, this is the nose base, this is the nose element, this is the faculty of the nose, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the nose base. ^1-604

What is the material form that is the nose base? The nose that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the nose, invisible and making impact, on which a smell that is invisible and makes impact has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is the nose, this is the nose base, this is the nose element, this is the faculty of the nose, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the nose base. ^1-605
