The nose that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the nose, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against a smell that is invisible and makes impact; this is the nose, this is the nose base, this is the nose element, this is the faculty of the nose, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the nose base. ^1-606

What is the material form that is the nose base? The nose that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the nose depending on which, on account of a smell, nose-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the nose depending on which, on account of a smell, feeling born of nose-contact… perception… intention… nose-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the nose depending on which nose-contact with a smell as its object has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the nose depending on which feeling born of nose-contact with a smell as its object… perception… intention… nose-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is the nose, this is the nose base, this is the nose element, this is the faculty of the nose, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the nose base. ^1-607

What is the material form that is the tongue base? The tongue that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the tongue, invisible and making impact, with which one has tasted, tastes, will taste or might taste a taste that is invisible and makes impact; this is the tongue, this is the tongue base, this is the tongue element, this is the faculty of the tongue, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the tongue base. ^1-608

What is the material form that is the tongue base? The tongue that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the tongue, invisible and making impact, on which a taste that is invisible and makes impact has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is the tongue, this is the tongue base, this is the tongue element, this is the faculty of the tongue, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the tongue base. ^1-609

What is the material form that is the tongue base? The tongue that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the tongue, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against a taste that is invisible and makes impact; this is the tongue, this is the tongue base, this is the tongue element, this is the faculty of the tongue, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the tongue base. ^1-610

What is the material form that is the tongue base? The tongue that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the tongue depending on which, on account of a taste, tongue-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the tongue depending on which, on account of a taste, feeling born of tongue-contact… perception… intention… tongue-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the tongue depending on which tongue-contact with a taste as its object has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the tongue depending on which feeling born of tongue-contact with a taste as its object… perception… intention… tongue-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is the tongue, this is the tongue base, this is the tongue element, this is the faculty of the tongue, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the tongue base. ^1-611

What is the material form that is the body base? The body that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the body, invisible and making impact, with which one has touched, touches, will touch or might touch a touch that is invisible and makes impact; this is the body, this is the body base, this is the body element, this is the faculty of the body, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the body base. ^1-612

What is the material form that is the body base? The body that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the body, invisible and making impact, on which a touch that is invisible and makes impact has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is the body, this is the body base, this is the body element, this is the faculty of the body, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the body base. ^1-613

What is the material form that is the body base? The body that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the body, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against a touch that is invisible and makes impact; this is the body, this is the body base, this is the body element, this is the faculty of the body, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the body base. ^1-614

What is the material form that is the body base? The body that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in one's own person, invisible and making impact; the body depending on which, on account of a touch, body-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the body depending on which, on account of a touch, feeling born of body-contact… perception… intention… body-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the body depending on which body-contact with a touch as its object has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the body depending on which feeling born of body-contact with a touch as its object… perception… intention… body-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is the body, this is the body base, this is the body element, this is the faculty of the body, this is a world, this is a door, this is an ocean, this is radiance, this is a field, this is a basis, this is the near shore, this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the body base. ^1-615

What is the material form that is the visible-form base? The visible form that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact: blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson [variant: mañjeṭṭhakaṃ (Sī., Syā.)], green, green-coloured, the colour of mango shoots, long, short, small, large, round, circular, square [variant: caturassaṃ (Sī., Ka.)], six-sided, eight-sided, sixteen-sided, low, high, shadow, sunshine, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust, the colour and appearance of the moon's disc, the colour and appearance of the sun's disc [variant: suriyamaṇḍalassa (Sī., Syā.)], the colour and appearance of the stars, the colour and appearance of a mirror's disc, the colour and appearance of gems, conch shells, pearls and beryl, the colour and appearance of gold and silver, or whatever other visible form there is that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact; the visible form, visible and making impact, that one has seen, sees, will see or might see with the eye, invisible and making impact; this is visible form, this is the visible-form base, this is the visible-form element – this is the material form that is the visible-form base. ^1-616

What is the material form that is the visible-form base? The visible form that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact: blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, green-coloured, the colour of mango shoots, long, short, small, large, round, circular, square, six-sided, eight-sided, sixteen-sided, low, high, shadow, sunshine, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust, the colour and appearance of the moon's disc, the colour and appearance of the sun's disc, the colour and appearance of the stars, the colour and appearance of a mirror's disc, the colour and appearance of gems, conch shells, pearls and beryl, the colour and appearance of gold and silver, or whatever other visible form there is that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact; the visible form, visible and making impact, on which the eye, invisible and making impact, has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is visible form, this is the visible-form base, this is the visible-form element – this is the material form that is the visible-form base. ^1-617

What is the material form that is the visible-form base? The visible form that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact: blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, green-coloured, the colour of mango shoots, long, short, small, large, round, circular, square, six-sided, eight-sided, sixteen-sided, low, high, shadow, sunshine, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust, the colour and appearance of the moon's disc, the colour and appearance of the sun's disc, the colour and appearance of the stars, the colour and appearance of a mirror's disc, the colour and appearance of gems, conch shells, pearls and beryl, the colour and appearance of gold and silver, or whatever other visible form there is that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact; the visible form, visible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against the eye, invisible and making impact; this is visible form, this is the visible-form base, this is the visible-form element – this is the material form that is the visible-form base. ^1-618

What is the material form that is the visible-form base? The visible form that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact: blue, yellow, red, white, black, crimson, green, green-coloured, the colour of mango shoots, long, short, small, large, round, circular, square, six-sided, eight-sided, sixteen-sided, low, high, shadow, sunshine, light, darkness, cloud, mist, smoke, dust, the colour and appearance of the moon's disc, the colour and appearance of the sun's disc, the colour and appearance of the stars, the colour and appearance of a mirror's disc, the colour and appearance of gems, conch shells, pearls and beryl, the colour and appearance of gold and silver, or whatever other visible form there is that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements, visible and making impact; the visible form on account of which, depending on the eye, eye-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the visible form on account of which, depending on the eye, feeling born of eye-contact… perception… intention… eye-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the visible form with which as its object, depending on the eye, eye-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the visible form with which as its object, depending on the eye, feeling born of eye-contact… perception… intention… eye-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is visible form, this is the visible-form base, this is the visible-form element – this is the material form that is the visible-form base. ^1-619

What is the material form that is the sound base? The sound that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the sound of drums, the sound of tabors, the sound of conches, the sound of tom-toms, the sound of singing, the sound of instruments, the sound of cymbals, the sound of hand-clapping, the noise of beings, the sound of things knocking together, the sound of wind, the sound of water, the sound of humans, the sound of non-human beings, or whatever other sound there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the sound, invisible and making impact, that one has heard, hears, will hear or might hear with the ear, invisible and making impact; this is sound, this is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is the material form that is the sound base. ^1-620

What is the material form that is the sound base? The sound that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the sound of drums, the sound of tabors, the sound of conches, the sound of tom-toms, the sound of singing, the sound of instruments, the sound of cymbals, the sound of hand-clapping, the noise of beings, the sound of things knocking together, the sound of wind, the sound of water, the sound of humans, the sound of non-human beings, or whatever other sound there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the sound, invisible and making impact, on which the ear, invisible and making impact, has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is sound, this is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is the material form that is the sound base. ^1-621

What is the material form that is the sound base? The sound that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the sound of drums, the sound of tabors, the sound of conches, the sound of tom-toms, the sound of singing, the sound of instruments, the sound of cymbals, the sound of hand-clapping, the noise of beings, the sound of things knocking together, the sound of wind, the sound of water, the sound of humans, the sound of non-human beings, or whatever other sound there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the sound, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against the ear, invisible and making impact; this is sound, this is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is the material form that is the sound base. ^1-622

What is the material form that is the sound base? The sound that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the sound of drums, the sound of tabors, the sound of conches, the sound of tom-toms, the sound of singing, the sound of instruments, the sound of cymbals, the sound of hand-clapping, the noise of beings, the sound of things knocking together, the sound of wind, the sound of water, the sound of humans, the sound of non-human beings, or whatever other sound there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the sound on account of which, depending on the ear, ear-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the sound on account of which, depending on the ear, feeling born of ear-contact… perception… intention… ear-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the sound with which as its object, depending on the ear, ear-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the sound with which as its object, depending on the ear, feeling born of ear-contact… perception… intention… ear-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is sound, this is the sound base, this is the sound element – this is the material form that is the sound base. ^1-623

What is the material form that is the smell base? The smell that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the smell of roots, the smell of heartwood, the smell of bark, the smell of leaves, the smell of flowers, the smell of fruits, the smell of raw flesh, the smell of rot, a pleasant smell, an unpleasant smell, or whatever other smell there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the smell, invisible and making impact, that one has smelled, smells, will smell or might smell with the nose, invisible and making impact; this is smell, this is the smell base, this is the smell element – this is the material form that is the smell base. ^1-624

What is the material form that is the smell base? The smell that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the smell of roots, the smell of heartwood, the smell of bark, the smell of leaves, the smell of flowers, the smell of fruits, the smell of raw flesh, the smell of rot, a pleasant smell, an unpleasant smell, or whatever other smell there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the smell, invisible and making impact, on which the nose, invisible and making impact, has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is smell, this is the smell base, this is the smell element – this is the material form that is the smell base. ^1-625

What is the material form that is the smell base? The smell that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the smell of roots, the smell of heartwood, the smell of bark, the smell of leaves, the smell of flowers, the smell of fruits, the smell of raw flesh, the smell of rot, a pleasant smell, an unpleasant smell, or whatever other smell there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the smell, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against the nose, invisible and making impact; this is smell, this is the smell base, this is the smell element – this is the material form that is the smell base. ^1-626

What is the material form that is the smell base? The smell that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the smell of roots, the smell of heartwood, the smell of bark, the smell of leaves, the smell of flowers, the smell of fruits, the smell of raw flesh, the smell of rot, a pleasant smell, an unpleasant smell, or whatever other smell there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the smell on account of which, depending on the nose, nose-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the smell on account of which, depending on the nose, feeling born of nose-contact… perception… intention… nose-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the smell with which as its object, depending on the nose, nose-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the smell with which as its object, depending on the nose, feeling born of nose-contact… perception… intention… nose-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is smell, this is the smell base, this is the smell element – this is the material form that is the smell base. ^1-627

What is the material form that is the taste base? The taste that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the taste of roots, the taste of stems, the taste of bark, the taste of leaves, the taste of flowers, the taste of fruits, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, tart [variant: lapilakaṃ (Sī.)], astringent, tasty, not tasty, or whatever other taste there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the taste, invisible and making impact, that one has tasted, tastes, will taste or might taste with the tongue, invisible and making impact; this is taste, this is the taste base, this is the taste element – this is the material form that is the taste base. ^1-628

What is the material form that is the taste base? The taste that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the taste of roots, the taste of stems, the taste of bark, the taste of leaves, the taste of flowers, the taste of fruits, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, tart, astringent, tasty, not tasty, or whatever other taste there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the taste, invisible and making impact, on which the tongue, invisible and making impact, has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is taste, this is the taste base, this is the taste element – this is the material form that is the taste base. ^1-629

What is the material form that is the taste base? The taste that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the taste of roots, the taste of stems, the taste of bark, the taste of leaves, the taste of flowers, the taste of fruits, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, tart, astringent, tasty, not tasty, or whatever other taste there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the taste, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against the tongue, invisible and making impact; this is taste, this is the taste base, this is the taste element – this is the material form that is the taste base. ^1-630

What is the material form that is the taste base? The taste that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact: the taste of roots, the taste of stems, the taste of bark, the taste of leaves, the taste of flowers, the taste of fruits, sour, sweet, bitter, pungent, salty, alkaline, tart, astringent, tasty, not tasty, or whatever other taste there is that is derived from the four great elements, invisible and making impact; the taste on account of which, depending on the tongue, tongue-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the taste on account of which, depending on the tongue, feeling born of tongue-contact… perception… intention… tongue-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the taste with which as its object, depending on the tongue, tongue-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the taste with which as its object, depending on the tongue, feeling born of tongue-contact… perception… intention… tongue-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is taste, this is the taste base, this is the taste element – this is the material form that is the taste base. ^1-631

What is the material form that is the faculty of femininity? Whatever, in a woman, is the feminine feature, the feminine sign, feminine conduct, the feminine manner, femininity, the state of being a woman [variant: itthittaṃ itthībhāvo (Syā.)] – this is the material form that is the faculty of femininity. ^1-632

What is the material form that is the faculty of masculinity? Whatever, in a man, is the masculine feature, the masculine sign, masculine conduct, the masculine manner, masculinity, the state of being a man – this is the material form that is the faculty of masculinity. ^1-633

What is the material form that is the faculty of life? Whatever, in those material states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is the faculty of life. ^1-634

What is the material form that is bodily intimation? Whatever, from a wholesome mind, an unwholesome mind or an indeterminate mind, in one who goes forward, goes back, looks ahead, looks around, bends or stretches, is the stiffening of the body, firm stiffening, the state of being firmly stiffened, intimation, making known, the state of having made known – this is the material form that is bodily intimation. ^1-635

What is the material form that is verbal intimation? Whatever, from a wholesome mind, an unwholesome mind or an indeterminate mind, is speech, utterance, way of speaking, uttering, voice, voicing, speech, breaking into speech – this is called speech. Whatever, through that speech, is intimation, making known, the state of having made known – this is the material form that is verbal intimation. ^1-636

What is the material form that is the space element? Whatever is space, what counts as space, void, what counts as void, an opening, what counts as an opening, untouched by the four great elements – this is the material form that is the space element. ^1-637

What is the material form that is lightness of material form? Whatever, in material form, is lightness, quickness to change, freedom from slowness, freedom from sluggishness – this is the material form that is lightness of material form. ^1-638

What is the material form that is pliancy of material form? Whatever, in material form, is pliancy, softness, freedom from hardness, freedom from rigidity – this is the material form that is pliancy of material form. ^1-639

What is the material form that is workability of material form? Whatever, in material form, is workability, being workable, the condition of being workable – this is the material form that is workability of material form. ^1-640

What is the material form that is growth of material form? Whatever is accumulation of the sense bases, that is growth of material form – this is the material form that is growth of material form. ^1-641

What is the material form that is continuity of material form? Whatever is growth of material form, that is continuity of material form – this is the material form that is continuity of material form. ^1-642

What is the material form that is ageing of material form? Whatever, in material form, is ageing, growing old, broken teeth, grey hair, wrinkled skin, the dwindling of life, the over-ripening of the faculties – this is the material form that is ageing of material form. ^1-643

What is the material form that is impermanence of material form? Whatever, in material form, is destruction, passing away, breaking up, falling apart, impermanence, disappearance – this is the material form that is impermanence of material form. ^1-644

What is the material form that is edible food? Boiled rice, porridge, barley meal, fish, meat, milk, curd, ghee, butter, oil, honey, molasses, or whatever other material form there is in whatever country that the beings there eat with the mouth, chew with the teeth, swallow down the throat and that fills the belly, the nutritive essence by which beings keep alive – this is the material form that is edible food.
This is the material form that is derived.
The analysis of derived material form.
The first recitation section of the chapter on material form. ^1-645

What is the material form that is not derived? The touch base, the water element. ^1-646

What is the material form that is the touch base? The earth element, the fire element, the air element, hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; the touch, invisible and making impact, that one has touched, touches, will touch or might touch with the body, invisible and making impact; this is touch, this is the touch base, this is the touch element – this is the material form that is the touch base. ^1-647

What is the material form that is the touch base? The earth element, the fire element, the air element, hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; the touch, invisible and making impact, on which the body, invisible and making impact, has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike; this is touch, this is the touch base, this is the touch element – this is the material form that is the touch base. ^1-648

What is the material form that is the touch base? The earth element, the fire element, the air element, hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; the touch, invisible and making impact, which has struck, strikes, will strike or might strike against the body, invisible and making impact; this is touch, this is the touch base, this is the touch element – this is the material form that is the touch base. ^1-649

What is the material form that is the touch base? The earth element, the fire element, the air element, hard, soft, smooth, rough, pleasant to touch, painful to touch, heavy, light; the touch on account of which, depending on the body, body-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the touch on account of which, depending on the body, feeling born of body-contact… perception… intention… body-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the touch with which as its object, depending on the body, body-contact has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise… the touch with which as its object, depending on the body, feeling born of body-contact… perception… intention… body-consciousness has arisen, arises, will arise or might arise; this is touch, this is the touch base, this is the touch element – this is the material form that is the touch base. ^1-650

What is the material form that is the water element? Whatever is water, what counts as water, moisture, what counts as moisture, the binding of material form – this is the material form that is the water element.
This is the material form that is not derived. ^1-651

What is the material form that is produced by past action? The eye base, the ear base, the nose base, the tongue base, the body base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action. ^1-652

What is the material form that is not produced by past action? The sound base, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action. ^1-653

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging? The eye base… the body base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging. ^1-654

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging? The sound base, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging. ^1-655

What is the material form that is visible? The visible-form base – this is the material form that is visible. ^1-656

What is the material form that is invisible? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is invisible. ^1-657

What is the material form that makes impact? The eye base, the ear base, the nose base, the tongue base, the body base, the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that makes impact. ^1-658

What is the material form that makes no impact? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that makes no impact. ^1-659

What is the material form that is a faculty? The faculty of the eye, the faculty of the ear, the faculty of the nose, the faculty of the tongue, the faculty of the body, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is a faculty. ^1-660

What is the material form that is not a faculty? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a faculty. ^1-661

What is the material form that is a great element? The touch base, the water element – this is the material form that is a great element. ^1-662

What is the material form that is not a great element? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a great element. ^1-663

What is the material form that is intimation? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation – this is the material form that is intimation. ^1-664

What is the material form that is not intimation? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not intimation. ^1-665

What is the material form that arises from mind? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation, or whatever other material form there is that is born of mind, caused by mind, arising from mind: the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that arises from mind. ^1-666

What is the material form that does not arise from mind? The eye base… the body base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not born of mind, not caused by mind, not arising from mind: the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that does not arise from mind. ^1-667

What is the material form that exists together with mind? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation – this is the material form that exists together with mind. ^1-668

What is the material form that does not exist together with mind? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that does not exist together with mind. ^1-669

What is the material form that accompanies mind? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation – this is the material form that accompanies mind. ^1-670

What is the material form that does not accompany mind? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that does not accompany mind. ^1-671

What is the material form that is internal? The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal. ^1-672

What is the material form that is external? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external. ^1-673

What is the material form that is gross? The eye base… the touch base – this is the material form that is gross. ^1-674

What is the material form that is subtle? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that is subtle. ^1-675

What is the material form that is far? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that is far. ^1-676

What is the material form that is near? The eye base… the touch base – this is the material form that is near. ^1-677

What is the material form that is a basis for eye-contact? The eye base – this is the material form that is a basis for eye-contact. ^1-678

What is the material form that is not a basis for eye-contact? The ear base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a basis for eye-contact. ^1-679

What is the material form that is a basis for feeling born of eye-contact… perception… intention… eye-consciousness? The eye base – this is the material form that is a basis for eye-consciousness. ^1-680

What is the material form that is not a basis for eye-consciousness? The ear base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a basis for eye-consciousness. ^1-681

What is the material form that is a basis for ear-contact… nose-contact… tongue-contact… body-contact? The body base – this is the material form that is a basis for body-contact. ^1-682

What is the material form that is not a basis for body-contact? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a basis for body-contact. ^1-683

What is the material form that is a basis for feeling born of body-contact… perception… intention… body-consciousness? The body base – this is the material form that is a basis for body-consciousness. ^1-684

What is the material form that is not a basis for body-consciousness? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a basis for body-consciousness. ^1-685

What is the material form that is an object of eye-contact? The visible-form base – this is the material form that is an object of eye-contact. ^1-686

What is the material form that is not an object of eye-contact? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not an object of eye-contact. ^1-687

What is the material form that is an object of feeling born of eye-contact… perception… intention… eye-consciousness? The visible-form base – this is the material form that is an object of eye-consciousness. ^1-688

What is the material form that is not an object of eye-consciousness? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not an object of eye-consciousness. ^1-689

What is the material form that is an object of ear-contact… nose-contact… tongue-contact… body-contact? The touch base – this is the material form that is an object of body-contact. ^1-690

What is the material form that is not an object of body-contact? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not an object of body-contact. ^1-691

What is the material form that is an object of feeling born of body-contact… perception… intention… body-consciousness? The touch base – this is the material form that is an object of body-consciousness. ^1-692

What is the material form that is not an object of body-consciousness? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not an object of body-consciousness. ^1-693

What is the material form that is the eye base? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the eye base. ^1-694

What is the material form that is not the eye base? The ear base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the eye base. ^1-695

What is the material form that is the ear base… the nose base… the tongue base… the body base? The body that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the body base. ^1-696

What is the material form that is not the body base? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the body base. ^1-697

What is the material form that is the visible-form base? The visible form that is colour and appearance derived from the four great elements… this is the visible-form element – this is the material form that is the visible-form base. ^1-698

What is the material form that is not the visible-form base? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the visible-form base. ^1-699

What is the material form that is the sound base… the smell base… the taste base… the touch base? The earth element… this is the touch element – this is the material form that is the touch base. ^1-700

What is the material form that is not the touch base? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the touch base. ^1-701

What is the material form that is the eye element? The eye base – this is the material form that is the eye element. ^1-702

What is the material form that is not the eye element? The ear base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the eye element. ^1-703

What is the material form that is the ear element… the nose element… the tongue element… the body element? The body base – this is the material form that is the body element. ^1-704

What is the material form that is not the body element? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the body element. ^1-705

What is the material form that is the visible-form element? The visible-form base – this is the material form that is the visible-form element. ^1-706

What is the material form that is not the visible-form element? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the visible-form element. ^1-707

What is the material form that is the sound element… the smell element… the taste element… the touch element? The touch base – this is the material form that is the touch element. ^1-708

What is the material form that is not the touch element? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the touch element. ^1-709

What is the material form that is the faculty of the eye? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the faculty of the eye. ^1-710

What is the material form that is not the faculty of the eye? The ear base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the faculty of the eye. ^1-711

What is the material form that is the faculty of the ear… the faculty of the nose… the faculty of the tongue… the faculty of the body? The body that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the faculty of the body. ^1-712

What is the material form that is not the faculty of the body? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the faculty of the body. ^1-713

What is the material form that is the faculty of femininity? Whatever, in a woman, is the feminine feature, the feminine sign, feminine conduct, the feminine manner, femininity, the state of being a woman – this is the material form that is the faculty of femininity. ^1-714

What is the material form that is not the faculty of femininity? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the faculty of femininity. ^1-715

What is the material form that is the faculty of masculinity? Whatever, in a man, is the masculine feature, the masculine sign, masculine conduct, the masculine manner, masculinity, the state of being a man – this is the material form that is the faculty of masculinity. ^1-716

What is the material form that is not the faculty of masculinity? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the faculty of masculinity. ^1-717

What is the material form that is the faculty of life? Whatever, in those material states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is the faculty of life. ^1-718

What is the material form that is not the faculty of life? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the faculty of life. ^1-719

What is the material form that is bodily intimation? Whatever, from a wholesome mind, an unwholesome mind or an indeterminate mind, in one who goes forward, goes back, looks ahead, looks around, bends or stretches, is the stiffening of the body, firm stiffening, the state of being firmly stiffened, intimation, making known, the state of having made known – this is the material form that is bodily intimation. ^1-720

What is the material form that is not bodily intimation? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not bodily intimation. ^1-721

What is the material form that is verbal intimation? Whatever, from a wholesome mind, an unwholesome mind or an indeterminate mind, is speech, utterance, way of speaking, uttering, voice, voicing, speech, breaking into speech, this is called speech. Whatever, through that speech, is intimation, making known, the state of having made known – this is the material form that is verbal intimation. ^1-722

What is the material form that is not verbal intimation? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not verbal intimation. ^1-723

What is the material form that is the space element? Whatever is space, what counts as space, void, what counts as void, an opening, what counts as an opening, untouched by the four great elements – this is the material form that is the space element. ^1-724

What is the material form that is not the space element? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the space element. ^1-725

What is the material form that is the water element? Whatever is water, what counts as water, moisture, what counts as moisture, the binding of material form – this is the material form that is the water element. ^1-726

What is the material form that is not the water element? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not the water element. ^1-727

What is the material form that is lightness of material form? Whatever, in material form, is lightness, quickness to change, freedom from slowness, freedom from sluggishness – this is the material form that is lightness of material form. ^1-728

What is the material form that is not lightness of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not lightness of material form. ^1-729

What is the material form that is pliancy of material form? Whatever, in material form, is pliancy, softness, freedom from hardness, freedom from rigidity – this is the material form that is pliancy of material form. ^1-730

What is the material form that is not pliancy of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not pliancy of material form. ^1-731

What is the material form that is workability of material form? Whatever, in material form, is workability, being workable, the condition of being workable – this is the material form that is workability of material form. ^1-732

What is the material form that is not workability of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not workability of material form. ^1-733

What is the material form that is growth of material form? Whatever is accumulation of the sense bases, that is growth of material form – this is the material form that is growth of material form. ^1-734

What is the material form that is not growth of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not growth of material form. ^1-735

What is the material form that is continuity of material form? Whatever is growth of material form, that is continuity of material form – this is the material form that is continuity of material form. ^1-736

What is the material form that is not continuity of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not continuity of material form. ^1-737

What is the material form that is ageing of material form? Whatever, in material form, is ageing, growing old, broken teeth, grey hair, wrinkled skin, the dwindling of life, the over-ripening of the faculties – this is the material form that is ageing of material form. ^1-738

What is the material form that is not ageing of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not ageing of material form. ^1-739

What is the material form that is impermanence of material form? Whatever, in material form, is destruction, passing away, breaking up, falling apart, impermanence, disappearance – this is the material form that is impermanence of material form. ^1-740

What is the material form that is not impermanence of material form? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is not impermanence of material form. ^1-741

What is the material form that is edible food? Boiled rice, porridge, barley meal, fish, meat, milk, curd, ghee, butter, oil, honey, molasses, or whatever other material form there is in whatever country that the beings there eat with the mouth, chew with the teeth, swallow down the throat and that fills the belly, the nutritive essence by which beings keep alive – this is the material form that is edible food. ^1-742

What is the material form that is not edible food? The eye base… impermanence of material form – this is the material form that is not edible food.
Thus material form is classified in two ways.
The exposition of the pairs. ^1-743

##### The Exposition of the Sets of Three ^1-2-3-3-0

What is the material form that is internal and derived? The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal and derived. ^1-744

What is the material form that is external and derived? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external and derived. ^1-745

What is the material form that is external and not derived? The touch base, the water element – this is the material form that is external and not derived. ^1-746

What is the material form that is internal and produced by past action? The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal and produced by past action. ^1-747

What is the material form that is external and produced by past action? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is external and produced by past action. ^1-748

What is the material form that is external and not produced by past action? The sound base, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is external and not produced by past action. ^1-749

What is the material form that is internal, and is produced by past action and subject to clinging. The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal, and is produced by past action and subject to clinging. ^1-750

What is the material form that is external and is bodily intimation? Whatever, from a wholesome mind, an unwholesome mind or an indeterminate mind, in one who goes forward, goes back, looks ahead, looks around, bends or stretches, is the stiffening of the body, firm stiffening, the state of being firmly stiffened, intimation, making known, the state of having made known – this is the material form that is external and is bodily intimation. ^1-847

Whatever, through that speech, is intimation, making known, the state of having made known – this is the material form that is external and is verbal intimation. ^1-850

What is the material form that is external and is not verbal intimation? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external and is not verbal intimation. ^1-851

What is the material form that is external and is not the space element? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external and is not the space element. ^1-854

What is the material form that is external and is not growth of material form? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external and is not growth of material form. ^1-869

What is the material form that is internal and is not continuity of material form? The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal and is not continuity of material form. ^1-870

What is the material form that is external and is continuity of material form? Whatever is growth of material form, that is continuity of material form – this is the material form that is external and is continuity of material form. ^1-871

What is the material form that is external and is not continuity of material form? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external and is not continuity of material form. ^1-872

What is the material form that is internal and is not ageing of material form? The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal and is not ageing of material form. ^1-873

What is the material form that is external and is ageing of material form? What is the material form that is external and is not ageing of material form? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is external and is not ageing of material form. ^1-875

What is the material form that is external and is impermanence of material form? Whatever, in material form, is destruction, passing away, breaking up, falling apart, impermanence, disappearance – this is the material form that is external and is impermanence of material form. ^1-877

What is the material form that is internal and is not edible food? The eye base… the body base – this is the material form that is internal and is not edible food. ^1-879

What is the material form that is external and is edible food? Boiled rice, porridge, barley meal, fish, meat, milk, curd, ghee, butter, oil, honey, molasses, or whatever other material form there is in whatever country that the beings there eat with the mouth, chew with the teeth, swallow down the throat and that fills the belly, the nutritive essence by which beings keep alive – this is the material form that is external and is edible food. ^1-880

What is the material form that is external and is not edible food? The visible-form base… impermanence of material form – this is the material form that is external and is not edible food.
Thus material form is classified in three ways.
The exposition of the sets of three. ^1-881

##### The Sets of Four ^1-2-3-4-0

What is the material form that is derived and produced by past action? The eye base… the body base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the space element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is derived and produced by past action. ^1-882

What is the material form that is derived and not produced by past action? The sound base, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the space element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is derived and not produced by past action. ^1-883

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is visible? Due to past action: the visible-form base – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is visible. ^1-922

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is invisible? The eye base… the body base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is invisible. ^1-923

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is visible? Not due to past action: the visible-form base – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is visible. ^1-924

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is invisible? The sound base, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is invisible. ^1-925

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and makes impact? The eye base… the body base, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and makes impact. ^1-926

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and makes no impact? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and makes no impact. ^1-927

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and makes impact? The sound base, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and makes impact. ^1-928

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and makes no impact? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and makes no impact. ^1-929

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is a great element? Due to past action: the touch base, the water element – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is a great element. ^1-930

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is not a great element? The eye base… the body base, the faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the space element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is not a great element. ^1-931

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is a great element? Not due to past action: the touch base, the water element – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is a great element. ^1-932

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is not a great element? The sound base, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the space element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is not a great element. ^1-933

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is gross? The eye base… the body base, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is gross. ^1-934

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is subtle? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is subtle. ^1-935

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is gross? The sound base, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is gross. ^1-936

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is subtle? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is subtle. ^1-937

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is far? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is far. ^1-938

What is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is near the eye base… the body base, or whatever other material form there is that is due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that is produced by past action and subject to clinging, and is near. ^1-939

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is far? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, ageing of material form, impermanence of material form, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the space element, the water element, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is far. ^1-940

What is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is near? The sound base, or whatever other material form there is that is not due to past action: the visible-form base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base – this is the material form that is not produced by past action but subject to clinging, and is near. ^1-941

What is the material form that makes impact and is a faculty? The faculty of the eye… the faculty of the body – this is the material form that makes impact and is a faculty. ^1-942

What is the material form that makes impact and is not a faculty? The visible-form base… the touch base – this is the material form that makes impact and is not a faculty. ^1-943

What is the material form that makes no impact and is a faculty? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life – this is the material form that makes no impact and is a faculty. ^1-944

What is the material form that makes no impact and is not a faculty? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation… edible food – this is the material form that makes no impact and is not a faculty. ^1-945

What is the material form that makes impact and is a great element? The touch base – this is the material form that makes impact and is a great element. ^1-946

What is the material form that makes impact and is not a great element? The eye base… the taste base – this is the material form that makes impact and is not a great element. ^1-947

What is the material form that makes no impact and is a great element? The water element – this is the material form that makes no impact and is a great element. ^1-948

What is the material form that makes no impact and is not a great element? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that makes no impact and is not a great element. ^1-949

What is the material form that is a faculty and is gross? The faculty of the eye… the faculty of the body – this is the material form that is a faculty and is gross. ^1-950

What is the material form that is a faculty and is subtle? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is a faculty and is subtle. ^1-951

What is the material form that is not a faculty and is gross? The visible-form base… the touch base – this is the material form that is not a faculty and is gross. ^1-952

What is the material form that is not a faculty and is subtle? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation… edible food – this is the material form that is not a faculty and is subtle. ^1-953

What is the material form that is a faculty and is far? The faculty of femininity, the faculty of masculinity, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is a faculty and is far. ^1-954

What is the material form that is a faculty and is near? The faculty of the eye… the faculty of the body – this is the material form that is a faculty and is near. ^1-955

What is the material form that is not a faculty and is far? Bodily intimation, verbal intimation… edible food – this is the material form that is not a faculty and is far. ^1-956

What is the material form that is not a faculty and is near? The visible-form base… the touch base – this is the material form that is not a faculty and is near. ^1-957

What is the material form that is a great element and is gross? The touch base – this is the material form that is a great element and is gross. ^1-958

What is the material form that is a great element and is subtle? The water element – this is the material form that is a great element and is subtle. ^1-959

What is the material form that is not a great element and is gross? The eye base… the taste base – this is the material form that is not a great element and is gross. ^1-960

What is the material form that is not a great element and is subtle? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that is not a great element and is subtle. ^1-961

What is the material form that is a great element and is far? The water element – this is the material form that is a great element and is far. ^1-962

What is the material form that is a great element and is near? The touch base – this is the material form that is a great element and is near. ^1-963

What is the material form that is not a great element and is far? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that is not a great element and is far. ^1-964

What is the material form that is not a great element and is near? The eye base… the taste base – this is the material form that is not a great element and is near. ^1-965

The visible-form base is seen, the sound base is heard, the smell base, the taste base and the touch base are sensed, and all material form is material form cognized by the mind.
Thus material form is classified in four ways.
The sets of four. ^1-966

##### The Set of Five ^1-2-3-5-0

What is the material form that is the earth element? Whatever is hard, what counts as harsh [variant: kharigataṃ (Ka.)], hardness, the state of being hard, whether one's own or others', produced by past action or not produced by past action – this is the material form that is the earth element. ^1-967

What is the material form that is the water element? Whatever is water, what counts as water, moisture, what counts as moisture, the binding of material form, whether one's own or others', produced by past action or not produced by past action – this is the material form that is the water element. ^1-968

What is the material form that is the fire element? Whatever is fire, what counts as fire, warmth, what counts as warmth, heat, what counts as heat, whether one's own or others', produced by past action or not produced by past action – this is the material form that is the fire element. ^1-969

What is the material form that is the air element? Whatever is air, what counts as air, the stiffened state of material form, whether one's own or others', produced by past action or not produced by past action – this is the material form that is the air element. ^1-970

What is the material form that is derived? The eye base… edible food – this is the material form that is derived.
Thus material form is classified in five ways.
The set of five. ^1-971

##### The Set of Six ^1-2-3-6-0

The visible-form base is material form knowable by the eye, the sound base is material form knowable by the ear, the smell base is material form knowable by the nose, the taste base is material form knowable by the tongue, the touch base is material form knowable by the body, and all material form is material form knowable by the mind.
Thus material form is classified in six ways.
The set of six. ^1-972

##### The Set of Seven ^1-2-3-7-0

The visible-form base is material form knowable by the eye, the sound base is material form knowable by the ear, the smell base is material form knowable by the nose, the taste base is material form knowable by the tongue, the touch base is material form knowable by the body, the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base and the touch base are material form knowable by the mind element, and all material form is material form knowable by the mind-consciousness element.
Thus material form is classified in seven ways.
The set of seven. ^1-973

##### The Set of Eight ^1-2-3-8-0

The visible-form base is material form knowable by the eye, the sound base is material form knowable by the ear, the smell base is material form knowable by the nose, the taste base is material form knowable by the tongue, an agreeable touch that is pleasant to touch is material form knowable by the body, a disagreeable touch that is painful to touch is material form knowable by the body, the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base and the touch base are material form knowable by the mind element, and all material form is material form knowable by the mind-consciousness element.
Thus material form is classified in eight ways.
The set of eight. ^1-974

##### The Set of Nine ^1-2-3-9-0

What is the material form that is the faculty of the eye? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the faculty of the eye. ^1-975

What is the material form that is the faculty of the ear… the faculty of the nose… the faculty of the tongue… the faculty of the body… the faculty of femininity… the faculty of masculinity… the faculty of life? Whatever, in those material states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is the faculty of life. ^1-976

What is the material form that is not a faculty? The visible-form base… edible food – this is the material form that is not a faculty.
Thus material form is classified in nine ways.
The set of nine. ^1-977

##### The Set of Ten ^1-2-3-10-0

What is the material form that is the faculty of the eye? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the faculty of the eye. ^1-978

What is the material form that is the faculty of the ear… the faculty of the nose… the faculty of the tongue… the faculty of the body… the faculty of femininity… the faculty of masculinity… the faculty of life? Whatever, in those material states, is lifespan, persistence, carrying on, keeping going, moving, continuing, protection, life, the faculty of life – this is the material form that is the faculty of life. ^1-979

What is the material form that is not a faculty and makes impact? The visible-form base… the touch base – this is the material form that is not a faculty and makes impact. ^1-980

What is the material form that is not a faculty and makes no impact? Bodily intimation… edible food – this is the material form that is not a faculty and makes no impact.
Thus material form is classified in ten ways.
The set of ten. ^1-981

##### The Set of Eleven ^1-2-3-11-0

What is the material form that is the eye base? The eye that is sensitivity derived from the four great elements… this is an empty village – this is the material form that is the eye base. ^1-982

What is the material form that is the ear base… the nose base… the tongue base… the body base… the visible-form base… the sound base… the smell base… the taste base… the touch base? The earth element… this is the touch element – this is the material form that is the touch base. ^1-983

What is the material form that is invisible, makes no impact and is included in the mental-object base? The faculty of femininity… edible food – this is the material form that is invisible, makes no impact and is included in the mental-object base.
Thus material form is classified in eleven ways.
The set of eleven.
The eighth recitation section.
The classification of material form.
The chapter on material form is complete. ^1-984

### The Chapter of Summaries ^1-3-0

#### Summary of the Sets of Three ^1-3-1-0

What are wholesome states? The three wholesome roots – non-greed, non-hate, non-delusion; the associated aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of mental formations, aggregate of consciousness; the bodily action, verbal action and mental action arising from them – these are wholesome states. ^1-985

What are unwholesome states? The three unwholesome roots – greed, hate, delusion; and the defilements that occur together with them; the associated aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of mental formations, aggregate of consciousness; the bodily action, verbal action and mental action arising from them – these are unwholesome states. ^1-986

What are indeterminate states? The results of wholesome and unwholesome states, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere, of the immaterial sphere or not included in the spheres of existence; the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, the aggregate of consciousness; and those states that are functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor results of action; all material form and the unconditioned element – these are indeterminate states. ^1-987

What are states associated with pleasant feeling? In the plane of pleasant feeling, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere or not included in the spheres of existence, except pleasant feeling; the associated aggregate of perception, aggregate of mental formations, aggregate of consciousness – these are states associated with pleasant feeling. ^1-988

What are states associated with painful feeling? In the plane of painful feeling, of the sense sphere, except painful feeling; the associated aggregate of perception, aggregate of mental formations, aggregate of consciousness – these are states associated with painful feeling. ^1-989

What are states associated with feeling that is neither painful nor pleasant? In the plane of feeling that is neither painful nor pleasant, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere, of the immaterial sphere or not included in the spheres of existence, except feeling that is neither painful nor pleasant; the associated aggregate of perception, aggregate of mental formations, aggregate of consciousness – these are states associated with feeling that is neither painful nor pleasant. ^1-990

What are states that are results? The results of wholesome and unwholesome states, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere, of the immaterial sphere or not included in the spheres of existence; the aggregate of feeling… the aggregate of consciousness – these are states that are results. ^1-991

What are states that produce results? Wholesome and unwholesome states, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere, of the immaterial sphere or not included in the spheres of existence; the aggregate of feeling… the aggregate of consciousness – these are states that produce results. ^1-992

What are states that neither are results nor produce results? Those states that are functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor results of action, all material form and the unconditioned element – these are states that neither are results nor produce results. ^1-993

What are states produced by past action and subject to clinging? The results, subject to taints, of wholesome and unwholesome states, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere or of the immaterial sphere; the aggregate of feeling… the aggregate of consciousness; and whatever material form is due to past action – these are states produced by past action and subject to clinging. ^1-994

What are states not produced by past action but subject to clinging? Wholesome and unwholesome states subject to taints, whether of the sense sphere, of the fine-material sphere or of the immaterial sphere; the aggregate of feeling… the aggregate of consciousness; and those states that are functional, neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor results of action; and whatever material form is not due to past action – these are states not produced by past action but subject to clinging. ^1-995
