What are states with an immeasurable object? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence and the four fruits of the contemplative life – these are states with an immeasurable object. The four arisings of mind dissociated from knowledge from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the four arisings of mind dissociated from knowledge from the functional mind, every unwholesome mind – these are sometimes states with a limited object, sometimes states with an exalted object, not states with an immeasurable object, and sometimes not to be counted among states with a limited object, nor among states with an exalted object. The four arisings of mind associated with knowledge from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the four arisings of mind associated with knowledge from the functional mind, the fourth absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome and functional), the functional mind-consciousness element without roots and accompanied by equanimity – these are sometimes states with a limited object, sometimes states with an exalted object, sometimes states with an immeasurable object, and sometimes not to be counted among states with a limited object, nor among states with an exalted object, nor among states with an immeasurable object. The three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the result of the fourth absorption, the base of infinite space, the base of nothingness – these are not to be counted among states with a limited object, nor among states with an exalted object, nor among states with an immeasurable object. Material form and Nibbāna are states without an object. ^1-1422

What are inferior states? The twelve arisings of unwholesome mind – these are inferior states. ^1-1423

What are middling states? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are middling states. ^1-1424

What are superior states? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are superior states. ^1-1425

What are states fixed in wrongness? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness – these are sometimes states fixed in wrongness, sometimes states that are not fixed. ^1-1426

What are states fixed in rightness? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence – these are states fixed in rightness. ^1-1427

What are states that are not fixed? The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not fixed. ^1-1428

What are states with the path as their object? The four arisings of mind associated with knowledge from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the four arisings of mind associated with knowledge from the functional mind – these are sometimes states with the path as their object, not states with the path as their root; sometimes states with the path as their dominant factor, and sometimes not to be counted among states with the path as their object, nor among states with the path as their dominant factor. The four noble paths are not states with the path as their object, but states with the path as their root; sometimes states with the path as their dominant factor, and sometimes not to be counted among states with the path as their dominant factor. The fourth absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome and functional), the functional mind-consciousness element without roots and accompanied by equanimity – these are sometimes states with the path as their object; not states with the path as their root, not states with the path as their dominant factor; and sometimes not to be counted among states with the path as their object. The four arisings of mind dissociated from knowledge from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, every unwholesome mind, all the results of the sense sphere, six arisings of mind from the functional mind, the three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the result of the fourth absorption, the four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional), and the four fruits of the contemplative life – these are not to be counted among states with the path as their object, nor among states with the path as their root, nor among states with the path as their dominant factor. Material form and Nibbāna are states without an object. ^1-1429

What are arisen states? The results of the four planes and whatever material form is due to past action – these are sometimes arisen states, sometimes states that are bound to arise; they are not to be counted among states that have not arisen. The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and whatever material form is not due to past action – these are sometimes arisen states, sometimes states that have not arisen; they are not to be counted among states that are bound to arise. Nibbāna is not to be counted among arisen states, nor among states that have not arisen, nor among states that are bound to arise. ^1-1430

Except Nibbāna, all states are sometimes past states, sometimes future states, sometimes present states. Nibbāna is not to be counted among past states, nor among future states, nor among present states. ^1-1431

What are states with a past object? The base of infinite consciousness, the base of neither perception nor non-perception – these are states with a past object. ^1-1432

There are no states that are invariably states with a future object. ^1-1433

What are states with a present object? The two sets of five kinds of sense consciousness and the three mind elements – these are states with a present object. Ten arisings of mind from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the mind-consciousness element accompanied by equanimity from the results of the unwholesome mind, the functional mind-consciousness element without roots and accompanied by joy – these are sometimes states with a past object, sometimes states with a future object, sometimes states with a present object. The wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the unwholesome mind, nine arisings of mind from the functional mind, the fourth absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome and functional) – these are sometimes states with a past object, sometimes states with a future object, sometimes states with a present object; and sometimes not to be counted among states with a past object, nor among states with a future object, nor among states with a present object. The three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the result of the fourth absorption, the base of infinite space, the base of nothingness, the four paths not included in the spheres of existence and the four fruits of the contemplative life – these are not to be counted among states with a past object, nor among states with a future object, nor among states with a present object. Material form and Nibbāna are states without an object. ^1-1434

Except material form not bound up with the faculties and Nibbāna, all states are sometimes one's own states, sometimes others' states, sometimes states both one's own and others'. Material form not bound up with the faculties and Nibbāna are others' states. ^1-1435

What are states with one's own states as object? The base of infinite consciousness, the base of neither perception nor non-perception – these are states with one's own states as object. ^1-1436

What are states with others' states as object? The three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the result of the fourth absorption, the base of infinite space, the four paths not included in the spheres of existence and the four fruits of the contemplative life – these are states with others' states as object. Except material form, all the wholesome, unwholesome and indeterminate states of the sense sphere, the fourth absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome and functional) – these are sometimes states with one's own states as object, sometimes states with others' states as object, sometimes states with both one's own and others' states as object. The base of nothingness is not to be counted among states with one's own states as object, nor among states with others' states as object, nor among states with both one's own and others' states as object. Material form and Nibbāna are states without an object. ^1-1437

What are states that are visible and make impact? The visible-form base – these are states that are visible and make impact. ^1-1438

What are states that are invisible but make impact? The eye base… the touch base – these are states that are invisible but make impact. ^1-1439

What are states that are invisible and make no impact? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, whatever material form is invisible, makes no impact and is included in the mental-object base, and Nibbāna – these are states that are invisible and make no impact.
The sets of three. ^1-1440

#### Explanation of the Pairs ^1-4-2-0

##### The Group on Roots ^1-4-2-1-0

What are states that are roots? The three wholesome roots, the three unwholesome roots, the three indeterminate roots. Non-greed as a wholesome root and non-hate as a wholesome root arise in the wholesome minds of the four planes. Non-delusion as a wholesome root arises in the wholesome minds of the four planes, except the four arisings of mind dissociated from knowledge from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere.
Greed arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed. Hate arises in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness. Delusion arises in all unwholesome minds.
Non-greed as a resultant root and non-hate as a resultant root arise in the results of the four planes, except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere. Non-delusion as a resultant root arises in the results of the four planes, except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere, and except the four arisings of mind dissociated from knowledge.
Non-greed as a functional root and non-hate as a functional root arise in the functional minds of the three planes, except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere. Non-delusion as a functional root arises in the functional minds of the three planes, except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere, and except the four arisings of mind dissociated from knowledge – these are states that are roots. ^1-1441

What are states that are not roots? Except the roots, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not roots. ^1-1442

What are states with roots? Except the delusion accompanied by doubt and the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere – these are states with roots. ^1-1443

What are states without roots? The delusion accompanied by doubt, the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the two sets of five kinds of sense consciousness, the three mind elements, the five mind-consciousness elements without roots, material form and Nibbāna – these are states without roots. ^1-1444

What are states associated with roots? Except the delusion accompanied by doubt and the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere – these are states associated with roots. ^1-1445

What are states dissociated from roots? The delusion accompanied by doubt, the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the two sets of five kinds of sense consciousness, the three mind elements, the five mind-consciousness elements without roots, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from roots. ^1-1446

What are states that are both roots and have roots? Where two or three roots arise together – these are states that are both roots and have roots. ^1-1447

What are states that have roots but are not roots? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere, except the roots arisen here – these are states that have roots but are not roots. States without roots are not to be counted – among states that are both roots and have roots, nor among states that have roots but are not roots. ^1-1448

What are states that are both roots and associated with roots? Where two or three roots arise together – these are states that are both roots and associated with roots. ^1-1449

What are states that are associated with roots but are not roots? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere, except the roots arisen here – these are states that are associated with roots but are not roots. States dissociated from roots are not to be counted – among states that are both roots and associated with roots, nor among states that are associated with roots but are not roots. ^1-1450

What are states that are not roots but have roots? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the results of the sense sphere, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes except the arisings of mind without roots from the functional mind of the sense sphere, except the roots arisen here – these are states that are not roots but have roots. ^1-1451

What are states that are not roots and are without roots? The two sets of five kinds of sense consciousness, the three mind elements, the five mind-consciousness elements without roots, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not roots and are without roots. States that are roots are not to be counted – among states that are not roots but have roots, nor among states that are not roots and are without roots. ^1-1452

##### The Short Intermediate Pairs ^1-4-2-2-0

What are states that have conditions? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that have conditions. ^1-1453

What are states without conditions? Nibbāna – these are states without conditions. ^1-1454

What are conditioned states? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are conditioned states. ^1-1455

What are unconditioned states? Nibbāna – these are unconditioned states. ^1-1456

What are visible states? The visible-form base – these are visible states. ^1-1457

What are invisible states? The eye base … the touch base, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, whatever material form is invisible, makes no impact and is included in the mental-object base, and Nibbāna – these are invisible states. ^1-1458

What are states that make impact? The eye base… the touch base – these are states that make impact. ^1-1459

What are states that make no impact? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, whatever material form is invisible, makes no impact and is included in the mental-object base, and Nibbāna – these are states that make no impact. ^1-1460

What are material states? The four great elements and the material form derived from the four great elements – these are material states. ^1-1461

What are immaterial states? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and Nibbāna – these are immaterial states. ^1-1462

What are worldly states? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are worldly states. ^1-1463

What are states that transcend the world? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states that transcend the world. All [variant: sabbeva (Syā.)] states are states that some kinds of consciousness can know and states that some kinds of consciousness cannot know. ^1-1464

##### The Group on Taints ^1-4-2-3-0

What are states that are taints? The four taints – the taint of sensual desire, the taint of existence, the taint of views, the taint of ignorance. The taint of sensual desire arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed, the taint of existence arises in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the taint of views arises in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view. The taint of ignorance arises in all unwholesome minds – these are states that are taints. ^1-1465

What are states that are not taints? Except the taints, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not taints. ^1-1466

What are states subject to taints? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states subject to taints. ^1-1467

What are states free of taints? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states free of taints. ^1-1468

What are states associated with taints? The two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, except the delusion arisen here; except the delusion accompanied by doubt and the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states associated with taints. ^1-1469

What are states dissociated from taints? The delusion arisen in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the delusion accompanied by doubt, the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from taints. ^1-1470

What are states that are both taints and subject to taints? Those same taints are states that are both taints and subject to taints. ^1-1471

What are states that are subject to taints but are not taints? Except the taints, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are subject to taints but are not taints. States free of taints are not to be counted – among states that are both taints and subject to taints, nor among states that are subject to taints but are not taints. ^1-1472

What are states that are both taints and associated with taints? Where two or three taints arise together – these are states that are both taints and associated with taints. ^1-1473

What are states that are associated with taints but are not taints? Except the taints, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states that are associated with taints but are not taints. States dissociated from taints are not to be counted – among states that are both taints and associated with taints, nor among states that are associated with taints but are not taints. ^1-1474

What are states dissociated from taints but subject to taints? The delusion arisen in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the delusion accompanied by doubt, the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from taints but subject to taints. ^1-1475

What are states dissociated from taints and free of taints? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from taints and free of taints. States associated with taints are not to be counted – among states dissociated from taints but subject to taints, nor among states dissociated from taints and free of taints. ^1-1476

##### The Group on Fetters ^1-4-2-4-0

What are states that are fetters? The ten fetters – the fetter of sensual lust, the fetter of aversion, the fetter of conceit, the fetter of views, the fetter of doubt, the fetter of misapprehension of rules and observances, the fetter of lust for existence, the fetter of envy, the fetter of stinginess, the fetter of ignorance. The fetter of sensual lust arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed. The fetter of aversion arises in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness. The fetter of conceit arises in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view. The fetter of views arises in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view. The fetter of doubt arises in the arisings of mind accompanied by doubt. The fetter of misapprehension of rules and observances arises in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view. The fetter of lust for existence arises in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view. The fetter of envy and the fetter of stinginess arise in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness. The fetter of ignorance arises in all unwholesome minds – these are states that are fetters. ^1-1477

What are states that are not fetters. Except the fetters, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not fetters. ^1-1478

What are states subject to fetters? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states subject to fetters. ^1-1479

What are states not subject to fetters? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not subject to fetters. ^1-1480

What are states associated with fetters? Except the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states associated with fetters. ^1-1481

What are states dissociated from fetters? The delusion accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from fetters. ^1-1482

What are states that are both fetters and subject to fetters? Those same fetters are states that are both fetters and subject to fetters. ^1-1483

What are states that are subject to fetters but are not fetters? Except the fetters, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are subject to fetters but are not fetters. States not subject to fetters are not to be counted – among states that are both fetters and subject to fetters, nor among states that are subject to fetters but are not fetters. ^1-1484

What are states that are both fetters and associated with fetters? Where two or three fetters arise together – these are states that are both fetters and associated with fetters. ^1-1485

What are states that are associated with fetters but are not fetters? Except the fetters, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states that are associated with fetters but are not fetters. States dissociated from fetters are not to be counted – among states that are both fetters and associated with fetters, nor among states that are associated with fetters but are not fetters. ^1-1486

What are states dissociated from fetters but subject to fetters? The delusion accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from fetters but subject to fetters. ^1-1487

What are states dissociated from fetters and not subject to fetters? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from fetters and not subject to fetters. States associated with fetters are not to be counted – among states dissociated from fetters but subject to fetters, nor among states dissociated from fetters and not subject to fetters. ^1-1488

##### The Group on Ties ^1-4-2-5-0

What are states that are ties? The four ties – the bodily tie of covetousness, the bodily tie of ill will, the bodily tie of misapprehension of rules and observances, the bodily tie of dogmatic belief that this alone is true. The bodily tie of covetousness arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed. The bodily tie of ill will arises in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness. The bodily tie of misapprehension of rules and observances and the bodily tie of dogmatic belief that this alone is true arise in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view – these are states that are ties. ^1-1489

What are states that are not ties? Except the ties, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not ties. ^1-1490

What are states subject to ties? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states subject to ties. ^1-1491

What are states not subject to ties? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not subject to ties. ^1-1492

What are states associated with ties? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, except the greed arisen here, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, except the aversion arisen here – these are states associated with ties. ^1-1493

What are states dissociated from ties? The greed arisen in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the aversion arisen in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from ties. ^1-1494

What are states that are both ties and subject to ties? Those same ties are states that are both ties and subject to ties. ^1-1495

What are states that are subject to ties but are not ties? Except the ties, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are subject to ties but are not ties. States not subject to ties are not to be counted – among states that are both ties and subject to ties, nor among states that are subject to ties but are not ties. ^1-1496

What are states that are both ties and associated with ties? Where view and greed arise together – these are states that are both ties and associated with ties. ^1-1497

What are states that are associated with ties but are not ties? The eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, except the ties arisen here – these are states that are associated with ties but are not ties. States dissociated from ties are not to be counted – among states that are both ties and associated with ties, nor among states that are associated with ties but are not ties. ^1-1498

What are states dissociated from ties but subject to ties? The greed arisen in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the aversion arisen in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from ties but subject to ties. ^1-1499

What are states dissociated from ties and not subject to ties? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from ties and not subject to ties. States associated with ties are not to be counted – among states dissociated from ties but subject to ties, nor among states dissociated from ties and not subject to ties. ^1-1500

What are states that are floods…. ^1-1501

##### The Group on Yokes ^1-4-2-7-0

What are states that are yokes…. ^1-1502

##### The Group on Hindrances ^1-4-2-8-0

What are states that are hindrances? The six hindrances – the hindrance of sensual desire, the hindrance of ill will, the hindrance of sloth and torpor, the hindrance of restlessness and remorse, the hindrance of doubt, the hindrance of ignorance. The hindrance of sensual desire arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed, the hindrance of ill will arises in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the hindrance of sloth and torpor arises in the prompted unwholesome minds, the hindrance of restlessness arises in the arisings of mind accompanied by restlessness, the hindrance of remorse arises in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the hindrance of doubt arises in the arisings of mind accompanied by doubt, the hindrance of ignorance arises in all unwholesome minds – these are states that are hindrances. ^1-1503

What are states that are not hindrances? Except the hindrances, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not hindrances. ^1-1504

What are states subject to hindrances? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states subject to hindrances. ^1-1505

What are states not subject to hindrances? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not subject to hindrances. ^1-1506

What are states associated with hindrances? The twelve arisings of unwholesome mind – these are states associated with hindrances. ^1-1507

What are states dissociated from hindrances? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from hindrances. ^1-1508

What are states that are both hindrances and subject to hindrances? Those same hindrances are states that are both hindrances and subject to hindrances. ^1-1509

What are states that are subject to hindrances but are not hindrances? Except the hindrances, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are subject to hindrances but are not hindrances. States not subject to hindrances are not to be counted – among states that are both hindrances and subject to hindrances, nor among states that are subject to hindrances but are not hindrances. ^1-1510

What are states that are both hindrances and associated with hindrances? Where two or three hindrances arise together – these are states that are both hindrances and associated with hindrances. ^1-1511

What are states that are associated with hindrances but are not hindrances? Except the hindrances, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states that are associated with hindrances but are not hindrances. States dissociated from hindrances are not to be counted – among states that are both hindrances and associated with hindrances, nor among states that are associated with hindrances but are not hindrances. ^1-1512

What are states dissociated from hindrances but subject to hindrances? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from hindrances but subject to hindrances. ^1-1513

What are states dissociated from hindrances and not subject to hindrances? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from hindrances and not subject to hindrances. States associated with hindrances are not to be counted – among states dissociated from hindrances but subject to hindrances, nor among states dissociated from hindrances and not subject to hindrances. ^1-1514

##### The Group on Misapprehension ^1-4-2-9-0

What are states that are misapprehensions? Misapprehension by view arises in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view – these are states that are misapprehensions. ^1-1515

What are states that are not misapprehensions? Except misapprehension, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not misapprehensions. ^1-1516

What are misapprehended states? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are misapprehended states. ^1-1517

What are states that are not misapprehended? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states that are not misapprehended. ^1-1518

What are states associated with misapprehension? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, except the misapprehension arisen here – these are states associated with misapprehension. ^1-1519

What are states dissociated from misapprehension? The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from misapprehension. Misapprehension is not to be counted – among states associated with misapprehension, nor among states dissociated from misapprehension. ^1-1520

What are states that are both misapprehensions and misapprehended? That same misapprehension is one of the states that are both misapprehensions and misapprehended. ^1-1521

What are states that are misapprehended but are not misapprehensions? Except misapprehension, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are misapprehended but are not misapprehensions. States that are not misapprehended are not to be counted – among states that are both misapprehensions and misapprehended, nor among states that are misapprehended but are not misapprehensions. ^1-1522

What are states dissociated from misapprehension but misapprehended? The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from misapprehension but misapprehended. ^1-1523

What are states dissociated from misapprehension and not misapprehended? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from misapprehension and not misapprehended. States that are misapprehensions and states associated with misapprehension are not to be counted – among states dissociated from misapprehension but misapprehended, nor among states dissociated from misapprehension and not misapprehended. ^1-1524

##### The Long Intermediate Pairs ^1-4-2-10-0

What are states that have an object? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes – these are states that have an object. ^1-1525

What are states without an object? Material form and Nibbāna – these are states without an object. ^1-1526

What are states that are mind? Eye-consciousness, ear-consciousness, nose-consciousness, tongue-consciousness, body-consciousness, the mind element, the mind-consciousness element – these are states that are mind. ^1-1527

What are states that are not mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not mind. ^1-1528

What are states that are mental factors? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – these are states that are mental factors. ^1-1529

What are states that are not mental factors? Mind, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not mental factors. ^1-1530

What are states associated with mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – these are states associated with mind. ^1-1531

What are states dissociated from mind? Material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from mind. Mind should not be described – either as "associated with mind" or as "dissociated from mind". ^1-1532

What are states joined with mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – these are states joined with mind. ^1-1533

What are states not joined with mind? Material form and Nibbāna – these are states not joined with mind. Mind should not be described – either as "joined with mind" or as "not joined with mind". ^1-1534

What are states arising from mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, bodily intimation, verbal intimation, or whatever other material form there is that is born of mind, caused by mind, arising from mind – the visible-form base, the sound base, the smell base, the taste base, the touch base, the space element, the water element, lightness of material form, pliancy of material form, workability of material form, growth of material form, continuity of material form, edible food – these are states arising from mind. ^1-1535

What are states not arising from mind? Mind, the remaining material form and Nibbāna – these are states not arising from mind. ^1-1536

What are states existing together with mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, bodily intimation, verbal intimation – these are states existing together with mind. ^1-1537

What are states not existing together with mind? Mind, the remaining material form and Nibbāna – these are states not existing together with mind. ^1-1538

What are states accompanying mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations, bodily intimation, verbal intimation – these are states accompanying mind. ^1-1539

What are states not accompanying mind? Mind, the remaining material form and Nibbāna – these are states not accompanying mind. ^1-1540

What are states joined with and arising from mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – these are states joined with and arising from mind. ^1-1541

What are states not joined with and arising from mind? Mind, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not joined with and arising from mind. ^1-1542

What are states joined with, arising from and existing together with mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – these are states joined with, arising from and existing together with mind. ^1-1543

What are states not joined with, arising from and existing together with mind? Mind, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not joined with, arising from and existing together with mind. ^1-1544

What are states joined with, arising from and accompanying mind? The aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of mental formations – these are states joined with, arising from and accompanying mind. ^1-1545

What are states not joined with, arising from and accompanying mind? Mind, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not joined with, arising from and accompanying mind. ^1-1546

What are internal states? The eye base… the mind base – these are internal states. ^1-1547

What are external states? The visible-form base… the mental-object base – these are external states. ^1-1548

What are derived states? The eye base… edible food – these are derived states. ^1-1549

What are states that are not derived? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, the four great elements and Nibbāna – these are states that are not derived. ^1-1550

What are states produced by past action? The results of the three planes and whatever material form is due to past action – these are states produced by past action. ^1-1551

What are states not produced by past action? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, whatever material form is not due to past action, the four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not produced by past action. ^1-1552

##### The Group on Clinging ^1-4-2-11-0

What are states that are kinds of clinging? The four kinds of clinging – clinging to sensual pleasures, clinging to views, clinging to rules and observances, clinging to a doctrine of self. Clinging to sensual pleasures arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed. Clinging to views, clinging to rules and observances and clinging to a doctrine of self arise in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view – these are states that are kinds of clinging. ^1-1553

What are states that are not kinds of clinging? Except the kinds of clinging, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not kinds of clinging. ^1-1554

What are states subject to clinging? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states subject to clinging. ^1-1555

What are states not subject to clinging? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not subject to clinging. ^1-1556

What are states associated with clinging? The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and associated with wrong view, the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, except the greed arisen here – these are states associated with clinging. ^1-1557

What are states dissociated from clinging? The greed arisen in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from clinging. ^1-1558

What are states that are both kinds of clinging and subject to clinging? Those same kinds of clinging are states that are both kinds of clinging and subject to clinging. ^1-1559

What are states that are subject to clinging but are not kinds of clinging? Except the kinds of clinging, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are subject to clinging but are not kinds of clinging. States not subject to clinging are not to be counted – among states that are both kinds of clinging and subject to clinging, nor among states that are subject to clinging but are not kinds of clinging. ^1-1560

What are states that are both kinds of clinging and associated with clinging? Where view and greed arise together – these are states that are both kinds of clinging and associated with clinging. ^1-1561

What are states that are associated with clinging but are not kinds of clinging? The eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed, except the kinds of clinging arisen here – these are states that are associated with clinging but are not kinds of clinging. States dissociated from clinging are not to be counted – among states that are both kinds of clinging and associated with clinging, nor among states that are associated with clinging but are not kinds of clinging. ^1-1562

What are states dissociated from clinging but subject to clinging? The greed arisen in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from clinging but subject to clinging. ^1-1563

What are states dissociated from clinging and not subject to clinging? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from clinging and not subject to clinging. States associated with clinging are not to be counted – among states dissociated from clinging but subject to clinging, nor among states dissociated from clinging and not subject to clinging. ^1-1564

##### The Group on Defilements ^1-4-2-12-0

What are states that are defilements? The ten bases of defilement – greed, hate, delusion, conceit, view, doubt, sloth, restlessness, shamelessness, fearlessness of wrongdoing. Greed arises in the eight arisings of mind accompanied by greed. Hate arises in the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness. Delusion arises in all unwholesome minds. Conceit arises in the four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view. View arises in the four arisings of mind associated with wrong view. Doubt arises in the arisings of mind accompanied by doubt. Sloth arises in the prompted unwholesome minds. Restlessness, shamelessness and fearlessness of wrongdoing arise in all unwholesome minds – these are states that are defilements. ^1-1565

What are states that are not defilements? Except the defilements, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not defilements. ^1-1566

What are states subject to defilement? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states subject to defilement. ^1-1567

What are states not subject to defilement? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not subject to defilement. ^1-1568

What are defiled states? The twelve arisings of unwholesome mind – these are defiled states. ^1-1569

What are undefiled states? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are undefiled states. ^1-1570

What are states associated with defilements? The twelve arisings of unwholesome mind – these are states associated with defilements. ^1-1571

What are states dissociated from defilements? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from defilements. ^1-1572

What are states that are both defilements and subject to defilement? Those same defilements are states that are both defilements and subject to defilement. ^1-1573

What are states that are subject to defilement but are not defilements? Except the defilements, the remaining unwholesome mind, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states that are subject to defilement but are not defilements. States not subject to defilement are not to be counted – among states that are both defilements and subject to defilement, nor among states that are subject to defilement but are not defilements. ^1-1574

What are states that are both defilements and defiled? Those same defilements are states that are both defilements and defiled. ^1-1575

What are states that are defiled but are not defilements? Except the defilements, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states that are defiled but are not defilements. Undefiled states are not to be counted – among states that are both defilements and defiled, nor among states that are defiled but are not defilements. ^1-1576

What are states that are both defilements and associated with defilements? Where two or three defilements arise together – these are states that are both defilements and associated with defilements. ^1-1577

What are states that are associated with defilements but are not defilements? Except the defilements, the remaining unwholesome mind – these are states that are associated with defilements but are not defilements. States dissociated from defilements are not to be counted – among states that are both defilements and associated with defilements, nor among states that are associated with defilements but are not defilements. ^1-1578

What are states dissociated from defilements but subject to defilement? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states dissociated from defilements but subject to defilement. ^1-1579

What are states dissociated from defilements and not subject to defilement? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states dissociated from defilements and not subject to defilement. States associated with defilements are not to be counted – among states dissociated from defilements but subject to defilement, nor among states dissociated from defilements and not subject to defilement. ^1-1580

##### The Closing Pairs ^1-4-2-13-0

What are states to be abandoned by seeing? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt – these are states to be abandoned by seeing. The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness – these are sometimes states to be abandoned by seeing, sometimes states not to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1581

What are states not to be abandoned by seeing? The arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1582

What are states to be abandoned by development? The arising of mind accompanied by restlessness – these are states to be abandoned by development. The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness – these are sometimes states to be abandoned by development, sometimes states not to be abandoned by development. ^1-1583

What are states not to be abandoned by development? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not to be abandoned by development. ^1-1584

What are states whose roots are to be abandoned by seeing? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, except the delusion arisen here – these are states whose roots are to be abandoned by seeing. The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness – these are sometimes states whose roots are to be abandoned by seeing, sometimes states whose roots are not to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1585

What are states whose roots are not to be abandoned by seeing? The delusion accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states whose roots are not to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1586

What are states whose roots are to be abandoned by development? The arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, except the delusion arisen here – these are states whose roots are to be abandoned by development. The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness – these are sometimes states whose roots are to be abandoned by development, sometimes states whose roots are not to be abandoned by development. ^1-1587

What are states whose roots are not to be abandoned by development? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the delusion accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states whose roots are not to be abandoned by development. ^1-1588

What are states with applied thought? The wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the unwholesome mind, eleven arisings of mind from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, two from the results of the unwholesome mind, eleven from the functional mind, the first absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome, resultant and functional), the first world-transcending absorption (wholesome and resultant), except the applied thought arisen here – these are states with applied thought. ^1-1589

What are states without applied thought? The two sets of five kinds of sense consciousness, the three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the four minds of the immaterial sphere (wholesome, resultant and functional), the three world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), applied thought, material form and Nibbāna – these are states without applied thought. ^1-1590

What are states with sustained thought? The wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the unwholesome mind, eleven arisings of mind from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, two from the results of the unwholesome mind, eleven from the functional mind, the one absorption of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the two in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the one world-transcending absorption in the fourfold method and the two in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), except the sustained thought arisen here – these are states with sustained thought. ^1-1591

What are states without sustained thought? The two sets of five kinds of sense consciousness, the three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the three in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional), the three world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the three in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), sustained thought, material form and Nibbāna – these are states without sustained thought. ^1-1592

What are states with rapture? The four arisings of mind accompanied by joy from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, four from the unwholesome mind, five from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, five from the functional mind, the two absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the three in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the two world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the three in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), except the rapture arisen here – these are states with rapture. ^1-1593

What are states without rapture? The four arisings of mind accompanied by equanimity from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, eight from the unwholesome mind, eleven from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, seven from the results of the unwholesome mind, six from the functional mind, the two absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the two in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional), the two world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the two in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), rapture, material form and Nibbāna – these are states without rapture. ^1-1594

What are states accompanied by rapture? The four arisings of mind accompanied by joy from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, four from the unwholesome mind, five from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, five from the functional mind, the two absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the three in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the two world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the three in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), except the rapture arisen here – these are states accompanied by rapture. ^1-1595

What are states not accompanied by rapture? The four arisings of mind accompanied by equanimity from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, eight from the unwholesome mind, eleven from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, seven from the results of the unwholesome mind, six from the functional mind, the two absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the two in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional), the two world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the two in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), rapture, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not accompanied by rapture. ^1-1596

What are states accompanied by happiness? The four arisings of mind accompanied by joy from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, four from the unwholesome mind, six from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, five from the functional mind, the three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the three world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), except the happiness arisen here – these are states accompanied by happiness. ^1-1597

What are states not accompanied by happiness? The four arisings of mind accompanied by equanimity from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, eight from the unwholesome mind, ten from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, seven from the results of the unwholesome mind, six from the functional mind, the fourth absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome, resultant and functional), the four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional), the fourth world-transcending absorption (wholesome and resultant), happiness, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not accompanied by happiness. ^1-1598

What are states accompanied by equanimity? The four arisings of mind accompanied by equanimity from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, six from the unwholesome mind, ten from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, six from the results of the unwholesome mind, six from the functional mind, the fourth absorption of the fine-material sphere (wholesome, resultant and functional), the four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional), the fourth world-transcending absorption (wholesome and resultant), except the equanimity arisen here – these are states accompanied by equanimity. ^1-1599

What are states not accompanied by equanimity? The four arisings of mind accompanied by joy from the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, six from the unwholesome mind, six from the results of the wholesome mind of the sense sphere, one from the results of the unwholesome mind, five from the functional mind, the three absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional), the three world-transcending absorptions in the fourfold method and the four in the fivefold method (wholesome and resultant), equanimity, material form and Nibbāna – these are states not accompanied by equanimity. ^1-1600

What are states of the sense sphere? The wholesome mind of the sense sphere, the unwholesome mind, all the results of the sense sphere, the indeterminate functional mind of the sense sphere and all material form – these are states of the sense sphere. ^1-1601

What are states not of the sense sphere? States of the fine-material sphere, states of the immaterial sphere, states not included in the spheres of existence – these are states not of the sense sphere. ^1-1602

What are states of the fine-material sphere? The four absorptions of the fine-material sphere in the fourfold method and the five in the fivefold method (wholesome, resultant and functional) – these are states of the fine-material sphere. ^1-1603

What are states not of the fine-material sphere? States of the sense sphere, states of the immaterial sphere, states not included in the spheres of existence – these are states not of the fine-material sphere. ^1-1604

What are states of the immaterial sphere? The four immaterial attainments (wholesome, resultant and functional) – these are states of the immaterial sphere. ^1-1605

What are states not of the immaterial sphere? States of the sense sphere, states of the fine-material sphere, states not included in the spheres of existence – these are states not of the immaterial sphere. ^1-1606

What are states included in the spheres of existence? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are states included in the spheres of existence. ^1-1607

What are states not included in the spheres of existence? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are states not included in the spheres of existence. ^1-1608

What are states that lead to liberation? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence – these are states that lead to liberation. ^1-1609

What are states that do not lead to liberation? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that do not lead to liberation. ^1-1610

What are fixed states? The four arisings of mind associated with wrong view, the two arisings of mind accompanied by sadness – these are sometimes fixed states, sometimes states that are not fixed. The four paths not included in the spheres of existence – these are fixed states. ^1-1611

What are states that are not fixed? The four arisings of mind accompanied by greed and dissociated from wrong view, the arising of mind accompanied by doubt, the arising of mind accompanied by restlessness, the wholesome mind of the three planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states that are not fixed. ^1-1612

What are surpassable states? The wholesome mind of the three planes, the unwholesome mind, the results of the three planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes and all material form – these are surpassable states. ^1-1613

What are unsurpassable states? The four paths not included in the spheres of existence, the four fruits of the contemplative life and Nibbāna – these are unsurpassable states. ^1-1614

What are states with conflict? The twelve arisings of unwholesome mind – these are states with conflict. ^1-1615

What are states without conflict? The wholesome mind of the four planes, the results of the four planes, the indeterminate functional mind of the three planes, material form and Nibbāna – these are states without conflict.
The explanation is complete.
The Enumeration of Phenomena is complete. ^1-1616
